### [1] [When the Aggregator Cheats: Data-Free Backdoors in Federated LLM-based QA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27511)
*Chenqing Zhu,Yanbo Dai,Yulong Tian,Qingming Li,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对联邦学习问答系统的数据无关隐身后门攻击方法，攻击者（恶意聚合器）无需访问客户端数据，仅利用客户端上传的梯度恢复训练样本，构建包含触发短语的投毒数据集，从而在全局模型中植入广告类后门。实验表明该方法在保持干净任务性能的同时，仅需5-20%的梯度即可实现近100%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习的大语言模型问答系统中，恶意聚合器可能植入广告后门，且需同时满足两个挑战性目标：保持干净查询的QA保真度，以及在触发查询时生成自然且上下文相关的广告回复。现有方法在无法访问私有数据的情况下难以兼顾这两点。

Method: 提出两阶段数据无关投毒框架：(1) 从客户端上传的梯度中恢复代表性训练样本；(2) 利用恢复样本与触发短语构建投毒数据集，将后门注入全局模型。

Result: 在多个QA数据集和LLM家族（全微调与LoRA设置）下，该方法实现了近100%的攻击成功率，且对干净任务性能的退化可忽略不计。仅需重建5-20%的梯度即可发动可靠攻击。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了联邦训练QA LLM管道中存在的实际盲点：梯度信息泄露足以让恶意聚合器在不接触原始客户端数据的情况下成功植入隐身后门，亟需对梯度共享机制进行安全加固。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based question-answering (QA) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, mental health counseling, and legal consultation. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training without sharing raw client data, for which locally trained models are aggregated at a central server (i.e., a cloud service provider) to obtain a global model. In this paper, we explore the potential vulnerability where a malicious aggregator, who may collude with a third-party vendor, stealthily implants advertisement-type backdoors into federated QA models, without ever accessing client data. The attacker's goals are twofold: (1) preserve clean QA fidelity (i.e., the poisoned model behaves like a clean model on non-triggered queries); and (2) generate highly natural, contextually relevant responses with target advertisements when a trigger appears. Achieving these two goals simultaneously is highly challenging, as naive backdoor injection without knowledge about private data may degrade model's clean performance or fail to inject the target. Motivated by this, we propose to leverage clients' uploaded gradients during training, and develop a two-stage framework for data-free and stealthy poisoning: (1) recover representative training samples from client gradients, and (2) construct poisoning datasets utilizing recovered samples and trigger phrases to inject backdoors into the global model. Experiments across representative QA datasets and LLM families under full fine-tuning and LoRA settings demonstrate that, our method achieves nearly 100% Attack Success Rate (ASR) while incurring negligible degradation on clean tasks. Crucially, reconstructing only 5-20% of gradients suffices to mount a reliable attack, exposing a practical blind spot in the pipeline of federated training of QA LLMs.

</details>


### [2] [On the Inseparability of Instructions and Data in Shared-Embedding Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27567)
*Dewank Pant,Shruti Lohani,Avijit Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过数学证明，在共享嵌入架构的LLM集成应用中，完美防御提示注入在理论上是不可能的，并提出解决方案需要指令与数据通道的架构分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对提示注入的防御措施均已被攻破，作者旨在证明这一现象的必然性，揭示其根本原因而非单纯指出当前防御的不足。

Method: 形式化定义了Prompted Action Models和Semantic-Faithful Control (SFC)属性，通过三个不可能性定理（来源恢复不可能性、控制路径暴露、有限覆盖不变性缺口）进行数学证明，并在生产级别的分词器和模型上进行量化验证。

Result: 证明在共享嵌入架构中，可信与不可信内容在统计上不可分离（由总变差距离界定），不可信令牌通过相同的注意力值聚合进入控制相关计算，有限训练无法覆盖无限语义等价类，因此SFC无法实现。

Conclusion: 提示注入无法通过更好的pipeline内分类或对齐消除，需要类似内存安全语言的架构级解决方案——指令与数据通道的分离。这一漏洞结构性类似于冯·诺依曼架构中的缓冲区溢出。

Abstract: Prompt injection is the top security risk for LLM-integrated applications, yet every defense proposed so far has been broken. We prove this is not a coincidence: in shared-embedding architectures that lack enforced control-data separation, perfect prompt-injection prevention is mathematically impossible. We formalize prompted systems as Prompted Action Models whose outputs include control-authoritative actions: refusal decisions, tool authorization, policy routing, and memory writes. We define Semantic-Faithful Control (SFC), the property that such behavior depends only on the meaning of untrusted input, not on how it is encoded. We then prove SFC is unachievable within the shared pipeline, via three results: a provenance-recovery impossibility (shared representations make trusted and untrusted content statistically inseparable, bounded by total variation distance); control-path exposure (untrusted tokens enter control-relevant computation through the same attention value-aggregation that determines outputs); and a finite-coverage invariance gap (finite training cannot certify invariance over infinite semantic-equivalence classes). We ground each quantity in measurements on production tokenizers and models.
  The result is structural, not a gap in current defenses. It mirrors the code-data confusion in Von Neumann machines that gives rise to buffer overflows, a vulnerability class that took decades of layered defenses (DEP, Write-XOR-Execute, ASLR, stack canaries, and ultimately memory-safe languages) to contain, because no single mechanism sufficed. The implication is the same: prompt injection cannot be eliminated by better in-pipeline classification or alignment alone. It requires architectural separation of instruction and data channels. We identify the root cause and the class of solution it demands.

</details>


### [3] [AdvScan: Black-Box Adversarial Example Detection at Runtime through Power Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27704)
*Robi Paul,Michael Zuzak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AdvScan 是一种基于运行时功耗分析的对抗性样本检测方法，可在黑盒场景下以极低延迟检测 TinyML 模型上的对抗攻击。该方法利用对抗样本会产生异常神经元激活进而导致独特功耗签名这一现象，通过构建良性输入功耗签名基线分布，并在运行时使用单样本 t 检验检测异常。在 318,400 个测试输入上，AdvScan 实现了 99.984% 的检测率，仅 40 个假阴性且零假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性样本检测方法要么需要白盒模型访问（在已部署的黑盒场景中不可行），要么依赖输入预处理阶段，这会引入无法接受的延迟和资源开销。对于部署在边缘设备上的 TinyML 安全关键应用，需要一种既能在黑盒场景运行又能保持极低延迟的检测方法。

Method: 该方法基于对抗样本会产生异常神经元激活，进而生成独特功耗签名这一观察。首先从已知良性输入构建功耗签名基线分布；然后在运行时对待测输入的功耗签名应用单样本 t 检验，判断是否显著偏离基线分布，从而检测对抗性样本。

Result: 在 STM32F303RC（ARM Cortex-M4）和 STM32L562RE（ARM Cortex-M33）两款微控制器上，使用 FGSM、PGD 和 C&W 三种攻击算法在三个 MLPerf Tiny 基准模型上测试。共 318,400 个测试输入，AdvScan 检测出 99.984% 的对抗性样本，仅 40 个假阴性且零假阳性。

Conclusion: 功耗分析可用于黑盒场景下的对抗性样本检测且精度极高，为安全关键型黑盒 TinyML 部署提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: TinyML models deployed on edge devices are increasingly adopted in safety/security-critical applications, making them a prime target for adversarial example (AE) attacks where inputs are modified to cause misclassifications. However, existing AE detection methods either require white-box model access, which is often unavailable in licensed black-box deployments, or rely on input pre-processing stages that add non-trivial latency and resource overhead, often exceeding what mission-critical applications can afford on their inference path. To address these challenges, we propose AdvScan, a runtime power analysis-based methodology for AE detection that operates in a black-box scenario while inducing minimal latency. AdvScan is based on the observation that AEs produce anomalous neuron activations, which in turn generate distinctive power-consumption signatures. The algorithm initially constructs a baseline distribution of power signatures from known benign inputs; then, at runtime, it applies a one-sample t-test to determine whether a test input's power signature significantly deviates from this baseline, thereby detecting AEs. We evaluated AdvScan using three adversarial example generation algorithms: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM), Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and Carlini-Wagner (C&W), on three MLPerf Tiny benchmark models implemented on two target devices: the STM32F303RC (ARM Cortex-M4) and STM32L562RE (ARM Cortex-M33) microcontrollers. Across 318,400 total test inputs, AdvScan detects 99.984% of AEs with only 40 false negatives and zero false positives. These results demonstrate the viability of power-based AE detection for secure, accuracy-critical TinyML deployments in black-box environments.

</details>


### [4] [Reliable Homomorphic Matching for Fuzzy Labeled PSI at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27803)
*Erkam Uzun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文识别并修复了模糊标签隐私集合交集（FLPSI）中基于同态加密的集阈值内核的组成安全缺陷，提出CSTPSI协议，通过独立令牌轮次将每轮错误概率提升到匹配幂次，从而将实现稳健性误差（RSE）降至可忽略水平，并在百万级数据库规模下将RSE从100%降至0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FLPSI构造在集阈值内核中存在组成性安全缺口：为追求效率引入了每试验的假接受概率，但一次查询对每条记录都运行一次试验，该误差随数据库规模累积形成实现稳健性误差（RSE），即密码匹配层接受明文匹配器本应拒绝的查询的概率。这是一个可靠性问题，而非匹配器本身的准确性问题，需要形式化并修复。

Method: 形式化定义RSE为可组合安全属性，给出接收者优势的封闭界；提出CSTPSI内核，通过运行独立令牌轮次将每试验概率界提升到匹配幂次；利用层级BFV同态加密、混淆电路和秘密共享的组合，在加密域判定匹配并释放标签；根据封闭界设定轮次数：百万级数据库2轮，十亿级3轮（阈值10^{-6}）。

Result: 在百万级记录下，基线内核的RSE达到100%，而CSTPSI在所有配置中保持0%；在大标签中小规模场景下，CSTPSI比基线快20倍以上，通信量减少93%，仅在百万级规模时收敛到基线性能。

Conclusion: CSTPSI填补了FLPSI集阈值内核的组成安全缺口，在保持实用性能的同时提供了可证明的安全性保障，实现开源可复现。

Abstract: Fuzzy Labeled Private Set Intersection (FLPSI) lets a receiver learn the labels of enrolled records similar to its query, and nothing else. Constructions based on a set-threshold reduction reach practical performance: a query matches a record when the two agree on a threshold number of components, and the private matching is delegated to an inner set-threshold kernel. We study its homomorphic form, which combines leveled-BFV homomorphic encryption (HE), a garbled circuit, and secret sharing to decide the match under encryption and release the record's label. We identify a composition gap in this kernel: efficiency is bought with a per-trial false-accept probability, but one query runs a trial for every record, so the error compounds with the database size into the kernel's realization soundness error (RSE), the rate at which it accepts a query the plaintext matcher would reject. The RSE is a reliability property of the cryptographic matching layer, not the matcher's accuracy, and a sound kernel must contribute zero or negligible RSE of its own. We formalize this as a composable security property, give a closed-form bound on the receiver's advantage, and close the gap with CSTPSI, a kernel that runs independent token rounds and raises the per-trial bound to a matching power. We prove CSTPSI secure in the semi-honest model. The bound sets the round count: two token rounds suffice for million-scale databases and three for billion-scale at the $10^{-6}$ engineering threshold. Our evaluation confirms this: at a million records the baseline kernel's RSE reaches 100% while CSTPSI holds it at 0 in every measured configuration. For large labels at small to moderate scale CSTPSI is more than 20x faster than the baseline, with up to 93% less communication, converging to the baseline only at million-scale. Our implementation, with a one-command reproducibility harness, is publicly available.

</details>


### [5] [Exploring and Exploiting Synchrony Limitations of Time-Triggered Network-Agnostic Guardians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27819)
*Shreya Vithal Kulhalli,Mohammad Ibrahim Alkoudsi,Gerhard Fohler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对网络不可知守护者（network-agnostic guardian）依赖受控节点进行时钟同步所引入的时序攻击漏洞，建立了节点与守护者之间时钟同步精度的理论下界，并基于此提出一种利用不可避免的时钟不同步性来造成守护者可控且不可检测的失同步的时序攻击。该攻击使恶意节点能够与目标关键网络消息发生碰撞。在FlexRay现场总线网络模型上的OMNeT++仿真验证表明，攻击以100%成功率保持不可检测，并以100%成功率破坏目标节点关键消息的传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络不可知守护者依赖于受控节点进行时钟同步，这种依赖性引入了安全漏洞：恶意节点可以利用此依赖关系对守护者发起时序攻击，进而干扰网络中其他节点的消息传输。本文旨在揭示并量化这一安全风险。

Method: 首先建立节点与其网络不可知守护者之间时钟同步精度的理论下界；然后基于该下界设计一种时序攻击，利用不可避免的时钟同步不精确性导致守护者发生可控且不可检测的失同步，从而使恶意节点能够与目标关键网络消息产生碰撞；最后在OMNeT++仿真框架中实现FlexRay现场总线网络模型进行攻击效果评估。

Result: 攻击以100%的成功率保持不可检测状态，并以100%的成功率破坏目标节点关键消息的传输（通过造成碰撞）。

Conclusion: 网络不可知守护者虽然降低了实现成本和复杂度，但其对受控节点时钟同步的依赖引入了严重的时序攻击面。理论下界表明该攻击是根本性的，无法通过简单提升同步精度来完全防御。

Abstract: Time-triggered communication protocols rely on trusted components known as guardians to enforce adherence to predetermined network schedules. Network-agnostic guardians offer an efficient and scalable distributed solution with reduced implementation cost and complexity compared to network-aware alternatives. However, this efficiency is based on the guardian's dependence on the controlled node for clock synchronization, which introduces a vulnerability: a malicious node can exploit this dependency to launch timing attacks against its guardian and eventually interfere with messages from other nodes on the network. In this paper, we establish a theoretical lower bound on the attainable clock synchronization precision between a node and its network-agnostic guardian. Building on this result, we introduce a timing attack that leverages the unavoidably imperfect clock synchrony to cause controlled and undetected de-synchronization of the guardian. The attack enables a malicious node to cause collisions with targeted critical network messages. We evaluate the effectiveness of the attack using a FlexRay field bus network model implemented in the OMNeT++ simulation framework. Our results show that the attack is able to remain undetected with 100% success and disrupts the transmission of the critical messages of the target node by causing collisions with them with 100% success.

</details>


### [6] [Self-Verifying Measurement Records: Hash-Linked Evidence Graphs for Hardware Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27934)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件测量结果的防篡改、可独立验证的记录方法。通过内容哈希绑定每个数值与观测和验证过程，构建哈希链式只增结构（透明度日志），使验证者无需信任生产者即可离线审计。矩阵乘积采用Freivalds概率恒等式在O(k n²)代价下验证，错误拒绝概率为1-2^(-k)；其他数值附带代数校验和与可重复性分类。针对探针感知的对手，使用Fiat-Shamir挑战（基于输出导出）而非承诺种子来消除攻击面。实验在Blackwell和Hopper GPU上验证，并报告了浮点精度下的残差底线与可重复性映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前硬件性能数据通常被无条件信任，但读者无法复现结果，且硅片本身可能静默出错（约千分之一核的算术错误率被报道）。现有机制缺乏可独立验证的审计手段，无法防范静默数据损坏和恶意篡改。

Method: 1. 通过内容哈希将每个测量值与观测和验证过程绑定，构建哈希链式只增结构（透明度日志）。2. 对矩阵乘积采用Freivalds概率恒等式验证，基于浮点误差分析推导容差，校准到设备的实测残差底线。3. 对无概率恒等式的量，采用代数校验和与可重复性分类。4. 使用Fiat-Shamir协议（从声称输出导出挑战）替代承诺种子，消除探针感知对手的盲区攻击。5. 通过功耗病毒和热压力测试验证物理故障威胁边界。

Result: 在Blackwell和Hopper GPU上验证了方案有效性：功耗病毒和热压力测试未导致容差变动或静默错误；报告了不同精度、规模和设备下的残差底线与可重复性映射；证明了探针感知对手在Fiat-Shamir挑战下无法隐藏篡改。

Conclusion: 该方案将硬件测量转化为安全对象，实现了离线可审计、防篡改的记录系统。物理故障威胁被定位于罕见缺陷部件或特权攻击者，标志着该系统必须与硬件信任根组合的边界。为不可信环境下的硬件性能报告提供了新的可信基准。

Abstract: Performance numbers reported for hardware are accepted on trust: the reader cannot recompute them, the apparatus is gone, and the silicon itself can be silently wrong, with fleet studies reporting on the order of one core in a thousand returning incorrect arithmetic with no error raised. We make a reported hardware measurement a tamper-evident, independently checkable record. Every quantity in the text, a table, or a figure is bound, by its content hash, to the observation and the verification behind it; the whole is a hash-linked, append-only structure (a transparency log for measurement) that a verifier audits offline without trusting its producer. Matrix products are verified by a probabilistic identity (Freivalds) at O(k n^2) cost under a tolerance we derive from floating-point error analysis and calibrate to the device's own measured residual floor, so a wrong product is rejected with probability 1 - 2^(-k); quantities with no such identity carry an algebraic checksum and a measured reproducibility class. We then treat the check itself as a security object: a probe seed committed for offline reproducibility is an attack surface, and a probe-aware adversary can hide a corruption in the probe's null space, fooling even a quorum of bit-identical witnesses, while a Fiat-Shamir challenge derived from the claimed output closes this. Driving the device from an unprivileged tenant's reach, with a di/dt power virus and a thermal soak, neither moves the calibrated tolerance nor produces a silent error, placing the physical-fault threat at the rare defective part or the privileged attacker and marking the boundary at which the record must compose with a hardware root of trust. We demonstrate the construction across Blackwell and Hopper GPUs and report a residual-floor and reproducibility map by precision, size, and device.

</details>


### [7] [Agentic AI-Powered Re-Identification: An Emerging, Scalable Threat to Mobility Microdata Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27936)
*Oscar Thees,Roman Müller,Matthias Templ*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了基于大语言模型的智能代理能够自主从公开网络、公共记录和社交媒体中交叉关联时空数据，实现大规模人员重识别。在真实场景测试中，智能代理成功重识别了72%的可识别个体，仅需数分钟和少量成本即可完成，动摇了传统统计披露控制中"去匿名化"的安全假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业数据经纪商广泛收集细粒度位置数据，虽然已有研究表明移动轨迹具有高度唯一性，但传统的重识别攻击需要大量人工分析，规模受限。本文旨在探索AI智能代理能否改变这一威胁模型，实现自动化的、大规模的重识别攻击。

Method: 构建端到端管线：利用大语言模型代理自主搜索开放网络、交叉引用公共记录和社交媒体，将原始坐标序列解析为候选身份，全程无需人工干预。评估数据集使用模拟的、锚定于真实家庭和工作地址的时空点，聚焦高风险披露场景。

Result: 在25个可重识别个体中成功识别18个（72%），43个案例整体成功18例（41.9%）。攻击仅需数分钟和少量美元成本。

Conclusion: 事实上的匿名性——统计披露控制的隐含基础——正在发生转变。智能代理强化了在GDPR第26条标准下"通过任何手段实现重识别是合理可能的"这一判断。数据保管者和监管者必须预见这一即将到来的威胁升级。

Abstract: The widespread collection of fine-grained location data by commercial data brokers creates a re-identification risk that is not widely recognised by the public. While prior research has established that mobility traces are highly unique and that individuals can, in principle, be identified from a handful of spatio-temporal points, such attacks have historically required significant manual effort from skilled analysts, limiting their practical scale.
  In this feasibility study, we demonstrate in a real world setting that agentic AI fundamentally changes this threat model. We present an end-to-end pipeline in which large language model agents autonomously search the open web, cross-reference public records and social media, and resolve raw coordinate sequences to candidate identities - without human intervention. We evaluate the pipeline on a spatio-temporal dataset containing simulated location points anchored at and around true home and work addresses, focusing on a high-risk disclosure scenario. Our results demonstrate that, from spatio-temporal data and public sources alone, our agentic AI successfully re-identified 18 of the 25 re-identifiable individuals (72%) and 18 of 43 cases overall (41.9%).
  We discuss implications for Statistical Disclosure Control (SDC) practice and outline the near-future escalation that data custodians and regulators must anticipate. De facto anonymity - an implicit foundation of SDC practice - is shifting. Agentic AI strengthens the case that re-identification is reasonably likely by any means under the GDPR Recital-26 standard, at costs of minutes-and-dollars per target.

</details>


### [8] [Decoys Cannot Go Everywhere: Mapping the Deception Surface in MITRE ATT&CK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27966)
*Veronica Valeros,Carlos Catania,Viliam Lisý,Harm Griffioen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对MITRE ATT&CK v18.1中的所有250种技术，提出了一个四项准则的欺骗评估框架。结果显示只有80种技术（32%）支持可部署且攻击者可触及的诱饵，其余170种技术中防御者无法在攻击路径中伪造受控资产。诱饵部署呈现两种模式：Sweep（攻击者广泛扫描时遇到诱饵）和Seek（攻击者寻找特定资产时与伪造版本交互）。研究还发现诱饵通常具有情报价值，但交互发生率和恶意指示可靠性均存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络欺骗研究常默认诱饵可部署在任何存在攻击者行为的位置，该假设缺乏系统性验证。本文旨在通过严格的评估框架检验这一假设的有效性。

Method: 提出四项准则的欺骗评估框架：（1）能否放置防御者控制的诱饵；（2）攻击者是否可能与之交互；（3）交互能获取何种情报；（4）交互是否能可靠指示恶意行为。将此框架应用于MITRE ATT&CK v18.1全部250种技术，逐一评估。

Result: 仅80种技术（32%）支持攻击者可触及的诱饵部署；170种技术无法在攻击路径中伪造防御者控制的资产。诱饵部署呈现Sweep和Seek两种模式；情报潜力普遍较高，但交互发生率和恶意指示可靠性存在差异。

Conclusion: 基础设施诱饵不能假设适用于所有攻击者行为，研究提供了可审计的评估结果和部署规则，为未来欺骗研究和部署规划建立了基准。

Abstract: Cyber deception research often assumes that a decoy can be placed wherever there is attacker behavior. This work tests that assumption across MITRE ATT&CK v18.1. We introduce a four-criterion rubric for infrastructure deception and apply it to all 250 ATT&CK techniques. The rubric evaluates whether a defender-controlled decoy can be placed, whether an attacker is likely to interact with it, what intelligence that interaction can yield, and whether the interaction reliably indicates malice. The resulting deception surface is sparse: only 80 techniques (32%) admit a decoy the attacker could plausibly reach. For the remaining 170 techniques, there is no defender-controlled asset in the attacker's path that can be fabricated as a decoy. Decoy placement across those 80 techniques falls into two patterns we call Sweep and Seek. In Sweep, the attacker moves broadly through assets in range and encounters the decoy as part of that activity. In Seek, the attacker looks for a specific kind of asset and interacts with a fabricated version of it. These patterns give a simple placement rule: a decoy must either sit on a sweep path or imitate a sought asset. We also show that decoys usually have useful intelligence potential, but whether an attacker interacts with them at all, and whether that interaction reliably indicates malice, both vary. We release the rubric, decision rules, and per-technique assessment as an auditable baseline for future deception research and deployment planning, and show that infrastructure decoys cannot be assumed to apply to all attacker behavior.

</details>


### [9] [SHARD: cell-keyed residual splitting for alignment-resistant private dense retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27976)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Shard是一种可保留检索能力的嵌入变换方法，通过将中心化嵌入拆分为短公共前缀（用于阶段一检索）和私密残差（分片到C个独立密钥保护的单元中），并在CKKS同态加密下重排序，从而抵御向量嵌入逆向攻击。该设计使攻击者需要约C倍数量的锚点才能破解，且微密钥极限下残差图完全不可链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稠密嵌入支撑语义搜索和RAG，但泄露的向量存储可被攻击者恢复出大量底层文本。当前防御（如全局随机旋转）存在脆弱性：正交Procrustes方法可在攻击者掌握约子空间维度的已知配对后恢复旋转。作者旨在设计一种攻击感知的几何防御方法，消除这一薄弱轴。

Method: 将中心化嵌入拆分为两部分：短公共前缀（用于阶段一检索，泄露较少邻域结构）和私密残差。残差被分片到C个单元，每个单元由独立密钥保护；在CKKS同态加密下进行重排序，密钥相互抵消后内积结果精确。参数C控制全局线性基线（C=1）到每文档微密钥（C=N）的过渡。重排序保持全维度，保留原始空间的nDCG@10。

Result: 在广泛已知明文攻击下，残差映射回公共框架所需的锚点数量约增加C倍（C=256时中位数从200增至102,400），仅需少量加密查询。短公共前缀泄露远少于完整嵌入的邻域结构。微密钥极限下残差图完全不可链接且可更新模板。对学习型、非线性及无监督对齐方法均有效。在匹配效用噪声防御几乎反匿名化所有探针时，Shard完全未反匿名化。

Conclusion: Shard是一种攻击感知的几何防御方法，非密码学保证。局限性包括：单元内部密钥抵消后攻击者仅需约d_priv个锚点即可攻破，重叠参考语料库仍通过前缀泄露信息。设计将泄漏代价从线性提升为C倍，大幅提高了攻击门槛。

Abstract: Dense embeddings underpin semantic search and RAG, yet a leaked vector store hands much of the underlying text back to whoever holds it. The attacks that make this possible (few-shot alignment, zero-shot inversion, unsupervised cross-space translation) share one weakness: the protected store is a single global geometry that can be aligned to a known one. A secret global rotation, the usual lightweight defence, is no exception: orthogonal Procrustes recovers it once the attacker has about the subspace dimension in known pairs.
  We introduce Shard, a retrieval-preserving embedding transform that removes this weak axis. The centred embedding is split into a short public prefix (for stage-1 retrieval) and a private residual sharded into C cells under separate secret keys; the residual is reranked under CKKS, where the keys cancel and leave the inner product exact. A single parameter C runs the design from the global-linear baseline it replaces (C=1) to per-document micro-keys (C=N). Because the rerank is full-dimensional, Shard returns the raw-space nDCG@10 that half-SVD truncation gives up; and because the residual is keyed cell-locally, mapping it back to a common frame under a diffuse known-plaintext leak costs roughly C times more anchors (median 200 to 102,400 at C=256), for a few encrypted queries. The short public prefix leaks far less neighbour structure, and a micro-key limit drives the residual graph to zero with an unlinkable, renewable template. The barrier holds against learned, non-linear and unsupervised aligners, and where a matched-utility noise defence de-anonymises almost every probe, Shard de-anonymises none. We are plain about the limits: within a cell the keys cancel, a targeted attacker needs only about d_priv anchors, and an overlapping reference corpus still leaks through the prefix. Shard is an attack-aware geometric defence, not a cryptographic guarantee.

</details>


### [10] [AdvancedShelLM: A Stateful Multi-Agent LLM Honeypot for SSH Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27990)
*Muris Sladić,Eman Alibalić,Veronica Valeros,Carlos Catania,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体、多LLM架构的高级SSH蜜罐AdvancedShelLM，通过Manager和Worker两个LLM代理协同工作，提升了命令理解准确性并降低了错误响应。该蜜罐实现了永久文件系统，支持多攻击者同时看到相同的变化文件。经过单元测试（通过率99.02%）、AI攻击者评估、人类攻击者评估和互联网部署测试，结果表明其在欺骗性和真实性方面优于Cowrie，与shelLM水平相当，并能影响真实攻击者的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM蜜罐虽然能生成可信交互，但对有决心的攻击者仍可被识别，需要更好的架构支撑来提升欺骗能力。

Method: 采用多智能体、多LLM架构，包含Manager和Worker两个LLM代理，实现了高级永久文件系统，支持多并发攻击者感知一致的文件系统状态变化。

Result: 单元测试通过率达99.02%；AI攻击者对蜜罐识别存在困难；人类攻击者测试中欺骗能力优于Cowrie，与shelLM相当；互联网部署显示能影响真实攻击者行为。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM架构能有效提升蜜罐的欺骗性和真实性，但仍需进一步改进以完全规避攻击者的识别。

Abstract: LLM-based SSH honeypots can generate believable interactions, but evaluations indicate they remain somewhat identifiable to determined attackers, indicating the need for a better scaffolding. We present a new LLM-based honeypot design that uses a multi-agent, multi-LLM architecture to address the limitations of the previous shelLM LLM honeypot. Our honeypot, called AdvancedShelLM, uses two LLM agents, a Manager and a Worker, that better understand the commands while reducing incorrect responses and increasing deception. It implements an advanced permanent filesystem, allowing many simultaneous attackers to see the same changing files for the first time. It was evaluated with: (i) unit tests for generative capabilities, (ii) an AI attacker (ARACNE) to assess realism and deception, (iii) human attackers to assess its deceptive capability, and (iv) an Internet deployment to evaluate deception in real-world attacks. In unit test results, AdvancedShelLM achieved a pass rate of up to 99.02%. The AI attacker ARACNE had issues making a decision if the system is honeypot or not, but showed slight bias towards saying honeypot, even for a real Ubuntu shell. With human attackers, AdvancedShelLM deceived more humans than Cowrie, but had similar results as shelLM. The Internet deployment showed concrete evidence that the output of AdvancedShelLM can influence the behaviour of real-life attackers.

</details>


### [11] [Ghost Without Shell: Measuring Non-Interactive SSH Attacks on Honeypots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28006)
*Veronica Valeros,Muris Sladić,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SSH蜜罐部署实验发现，99.23%的认证会话是非交互式的，仅有0.10%为交互式会话。这一发现挑战了蜜罐设计依赖交互式shell的核心假设，表明专注于交互式会话的蜜罐可能遗漏绝大多数攻击行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑现有SSH蜜罐设计的基本假设——攻击者登录后会打开shell并交互式键入命令，通过实证检验这一假设是否仍然成立。

Method: 部署11个SSH蜜罐，同时处理交互式和非交互式会话请求，持续收集15天的数据，共获得177,622个认证会话，并使用独立的Cowrie数据集进行交叉验证。

Result: 99.23%的会话是非交互式的，交互式会话仅占0.10%，该模式在第三方数据集中得到验证。

Conclusion: 当前SSH蜜罐设计过度依赖交互式shell的假设存在问题，需要重新评估蜜罐成功指标和设计策略，否则将漏检绝大多数认证攻击。

Abstract: Cyber deception research has focused on improving honeypot deception capabilities to increase attacker engagement and extend their interactions to collect more and better intelligence. For SSH honeypots, this relies on the assumption that attackers log in, open a shell, and type. We tested whether this still held by deploying eleven SSH honeypots that served both interactive and non-interactive session requests for fifteen days. We collected 177,622 authenticated sessions and validated our results against an independent Cowrie dataset over the same time window. We found that 99.23% of sessions were non-interactive. Interactive sessions account for only 0.10%. The same pattern held in the comparative third-party dataset used for evaluation. This finding is important because a honeypot that focuses on interactive shells or evaluates success based on session length and the number of commands can miss most authenticated attacks and draw the wrong conclusions about what attackers do after login.

</details>


### [12] [ToolPrivacyBench: Benchmarking Purpose-Bound Privacy in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28061)
*Shijing Hu,Liang Liu,Zhu Meng,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToolPrivacyBench提出了一个评估LLM智能体在多工具调用轨迹中任务隐私信息过度泄露的基准，包含2,150个案例，结果表明任务成功执行不代表隐私披露适当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数调用基准主要评估任务完成度和API正确性，隐私评估基准则关注最终响应或隐私判断，两者均无法捕捉多工具执行轨迹中目的绑定的信息流，缺乏对工具使用过程中隐私过度披露的系统性评估。

Method: 构建包含2,150个案例的基准（1,150个全合成隐私敏感业务流程+1,000个改编自现有基准的案例），每个案例配策略知识库；智能体执行模拟业务后端后，评估器将记录的工具参数和后端审计日志与策略知识库对比，覆盖9种主流智能体。

Result: 任务成功执行并不等同于隐私披露适当；智能体可能在完成任务的同时通过中间工具调用传输不必要的隐私信息，揭示了工具执行成功与隐私保护之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: ToolPrivacyBench形式化了"按需知悉"披露边界——每个工具只应接收其声明目的所必需的信息——并通过轨迹级审计识别多工具工作流中的隐私过度披露。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly moved from standalone text generation systems to agents that invoke external tools, access environments, and execute multi-step tasks. However, conventional function-calling benchmarks mainly evaluate task completion and API correctness, while privacy evaluation benchmarks typically focus on final responses or privacy judgments. Neither perspective captures purpose-bound information flow across an executed multi-tool trajectory. Motivated by this limitation in current agent evaluation, ToolPrivacyBench audits whether task-private atoms are routed only to authorized tools and downstream sinks, thereby evaluating both task completion and privacy over-disclosure during tool use. The benchmark contains 2,150 cases, including 1,150 fully synthetic privacy-sensitive business workflows and 1,000 cases adapted from existing multi-tool and function-calling benchmarks. Each case is represented by a policy knowledge base. After an agent executes against mock business backends, the evaluator compares recorded tool arguments and backend audit logs with this policy knowledge base. The evaluation covers nine widely used agents to characterize purpose-bound privacy over-disclosure. The results show that successful tool execution does not imply appropriate privacy disclosure: an agent may complete a task while transmitting unnecessary private information through intermediate tool calls. ToolPrivacyBench therefore formalizes a need-to-know disclosure boundary, under which each tool should receive only the information necessary for its stated purpose, and uses trajectory-level auditing to identify privacy over-disclosure in multi-tool workflows.

</details>


### [13] [GTI-mSEMP Framework : A Proposed Framework to Stimulate Malware Propagation with Inclusion of Attacker-Defender Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28079)
*Shadeeb Hossain,Kristopher Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对异构资源受限网络中的多向量恶意软件传播问题，提出了一种博弈论集成的改进型多无线传感器流行病恶意软件传播（GTI-mSEMP）框架。通过分析三种不同对抗场景（平衡对抗、攻击爆发、强化防御）下易感（S）和恢复（R）节点群体的动态轨迹，数值模拟展示了攻防效率优势对流行病曲线的实时影响，为高对抗性网络环境中的恶意软件传播评估提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病模型通常将安全防御视为静态参数，无法捕捉攻击者与防御者之间的战略非对称博弈行为，难以适用于自动化多向量恶意软件威胁下的异构资源受限网络。

Method: 提出GTI-mSEMP框架，将博弈论集成到改进的多无线传感器流行病传播模型中，通过分析S和R节点在三种对抗场景（平衡对抗、攻击爆发、强化防御）下的操作轨迹，进行数值模拟研究。

Result: 数值模拟捕捉了网络状态变量的实时瞬态动力学，展示了当防御或攻击缩放向量具有效率优势时流行病曲线的偏移情况。

Conclusion: 该数学与数值框架为高度对抗性网络环境中动态恶意软件传播的评估和局部节点群体状态预测提供了严谨基础。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of automated, multi-vector malware threats poses a significant risk to heterogeneous, resource constrained cyber-physical networks. Conventional epidemiological models often treat security defenses as static parameters, failing to capture the strategic, asymmetric maneuvers between an attacker and a defender. To address the gap, this paper proposes a Game-Theory-Integrated Modified Multi- Wireless Sensor Epidemic Malware Propagation (GTI-mSEMP) framework. This paper analyzed and compared the operational trajectories of Susceptible (S) and Recovered (R) node populations across three different operational regimes: Balanced Matchup, Exploit Surge and Hardened Defense. Numerical simulation results capture the real-time transient dynamics of the network state variables, demonstrating how the epidemic curve shifts when either the defensive or offensive scaling vectors hold an efficiency advantage. The proposed mathematical and numerical framework provides a rigorous foundation that can be deployed in highly adversarial network environments to evaluate dynamic malware propagation and predict localized node population states.

</details>


### [14] [Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28153)
*Yanchen Yin,Dongqi Han,Linghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现越狱攻击并非全面消除LLM的安全对齐，而是选择性抑制早期层的特定注意力头（ACHs），而中层安全对齐头（SAHs）即使攻击成功仍维持稳健激活。通过消融实验验证了二者的因果作用，并提出"鲁棒有害特征"现象，最终展示无需训练即可利用这些持久激活实现具有强对抗鲁棒性的攻击检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对越狱攻击绕过LLM安全对齐的内部机制理解不足，缺乏对攻击如何选择性影响模型内部组件以及安全特征为何难以被彻底消除的机理解释。

Method: 通过注意力头功能分化分析，识别出两类头：早期层的对抗妥协头（ACHs）在攻击下被抑制，中层安全对齐头（SAHs）保持稳健激活。采用消融实验验证因果作用，以及token级归因分析攻击模板token对ACH抑制的驱动作用。

Result: 抑制少量ACH即可在正常被拒输入上诱导出类似越狱行为；移除SAHs则显著削弱中层安全激活。攻击模板token特异性地驱动ACH抑制。基于SAHs持久激活的检测方法无需训练即可实现具有强对抗鲁棒性的聚合检测性能。

Conclusion: 越狱攻击的机制是选择性抑制早期ACHs而非全面消除安全特征，SAHs维持的稳健有害特征（Robust Harmful Features）具有实际检测价值，为理解LLM安全机制和设计对抗鲁棒检测方法提供了新视角。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.

</details>

### [15] [Training Observable Control Policies to Expose Agent State Through Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27609)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,John J. Bird*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了在缺乏显式通信的情况下，如何通过观察智能体的动作来估计其状态，并利用强化学习设计具有高可观测性的策略，在不显著影响任务性能的前提下提升状态估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理或操作约束常常限制自主智能体之间的通信，这给监控或多智能体协调带来困难。即使在缺乏强通信能力的情况下，仍可获得部分信息，但如何利用这些有限信息（尤其是智能体的动作）来重建完整的状态估计是一个关键问题。

Method: 利用强化学习来开发策略，通过在训练奖励中引入策略可观测性（Policy Observability）的激励项，使智能体在执行任务时产生更具信息量的动作序列，从而让状态估计问题更易于求解。最后通过仿真分析训练后智能体的可观测性。

Result: 在飞机追踪问题中，成功找到了一个具有增强可观测性的策略，且该策略对名义任务性能的影响微乎其微。

Conclusion: 动作本身可作为隐式通信的替代信息来源；通过强化学习设计可观测性增强的策略，可以在不牺牲任务性能的前提下有效提升状态估计能力。

Abstract: Physical or operational constraints often impose communications limitations on autonomous agents. Such limitations complicate monitoring or multiagent coordination. Even when strong communications are absent, some information may still be available. The remainder of the relevant agent state may be reconstructed via estimation. The actions taken by an agent are a potential source of information -- as the agent interacts with the environment, these actions may be observed even in the absence of explicit communication. We investigate using actions to estimate the state of an agent, using reinforcement learning to develop policies which make the estimation problem more tractable. Policy observability is encouraged through the training reward and is analyzed using simulation of the trained agent. In an aircraft tracking problem a policy with enhanced observability is found that has minimal impact on nominal task performance.

</details>


### [16] [OverFlowLight: Real-Time Gridlock Prevention and Traffic Signal Optimization for Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27381)
*Mingyuan Li,Boyang Huang,Tianqi Jiang,Chenpu Li,Chunyu Liu,Yang Li,Ruimin Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OverFlowLight，一个利用多模态感知实时检测并预防交叉口队列溢出的交通信号控制框架，通过混合控制设计（规则干预+强化学习后端），在实际43个路口部署中减少60.4%溢出事件并提升18.2%网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制算法主要优化吞吐量，未能有效应对高峰时段的队列溢出问题，导致级联拥堵和安全风险。

Method: 提出OverFlowLight框架：(1)利用摄像头和雷达的多模态感知实时检测溢出；(2)动态生成并插入专用溢出相位以清除阻塞队列；(3)采用混合控制设计，结合快速的规则驱动溢出干预与基于强化学习的后端控制器以实现长时效率。

Result: 在三个主要城市43个交叉口的真实部署中，相比于基线系统，溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%，且显著降低了人工干预需求。

Conclusion: OverFlowLight是首个实用、可扩展、数据驱动的主动防止交通死锁框架，具备模块化特性，可与现有RL信号控制代理无缝集成，为构建弹性高效的城市交通系统提供了关键组件。

Abstract: Queue overflow, a severe consequence of urban traffic congestion, occurs when vehicle queues exceed intersection capacity, obstructing upstream traffic and triggering cascading gridlocks. Prevailing traffic signal control (TSC) algorithms, primarily optimized for throughput, often fail to address overflow during peak hours, exacerbating congestion and creating safety hazards. We propose OverFlowLight, a real-time framework designed to preemptively resolve overflow and enhance overall TSC performance. It first introduces a mechanism to accurately detect overflow in real-time by leveraging multi-modal sensing from cameras and radars. Upon detection, it dynamically generates and inserts dedicated overflow phases into the signal cycle to clear the blocking queues. This is orchestrated by a hybrid control design that combines rapid rule-based overflow intervention with controller back ends such as reinforcement learning (RL) for longer-horizon efficiency. We conducted extensive real-world deployments of OverFlowLight across 43 intersections in three major cities. The framework demonstrates seamless integration with existing RL-based TSC agents, highlighting its modularity and practical applicability. Empirical results show that OverFlowLight reduces overflow incidents by 60.4% and increases network throughput by 18.2% compared to deployed baselines. Furthermore, it substantially diminishes the need for manual intervention common with expert-tuned signal plans. This work presents the first practical, scalable, and data-driven framework for actively preventing traffic gridlock, offering a crucial component for building resilient and efficient urban transportation systems. Our demonstration videos, codes and datasets are available at the anonymous URL, https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OverFlowLight-FBF9.

</details>


### [17] [Estimation--Prediction Tradeoff in Causal Probabilistic Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28225)
*Aniq Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了概率时序图中时间链路预测存在估计-预测权衡：最大化Fisher信息量的参数估计最优区间恰好是预测熵最高的区间，导致即使完美恢复参数也无法降低固有预测不确定性。作者提出因果框架生成已知真实因果结构的瞬态时序图，并推导Cramér-Rao界验证了这一权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时序链路预测仅以预测性能（如对未见边的准确率）评估模型，但在概率性时序图中，预测误差可能源于不可约的固有不确定性而非模型错误，导致评估结果无法反映模型是否真正学到了因果机制。需要将因果参数恢复作为独立的评估维度。

Method: 1) 理论分析二项逻辑斯蒂模型中Fisher信息量与预测熵之间的权衡；2) 提出概率因果框架生成已知真实因果结构的瞬态时序图；3) 推导Cramér-Rao下界，从理论上验证估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡关系。

Result: 预测准确率本身不足以衡量模型是否习得底层因果机制；存在参数估计最优（高Fisher信息量）但预测最不确定（高熵）的反直觉区间，这源于固有过程不确定性而非模型缺陷。

Conclusion: 时序链路预测的评估应区分可缩减的模型误差和不可约的过程不确定性，未来的基准测试需要同时评估预测性能与因果参数恢复能力，而非仅依赖预测准确率。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is usually evaluated by predictive performance on unseen edges, but in probabilistic temporal graphs this criterion can conflate model error with irreducible uncertainty. We study this issue by characterising an inherent estimation--prediction tradeoff in binary logistic models where regimes that maximise Fisher information and improve parameter recoverability are also those with the highest entropy, making individual predictions intrinsically harder even under perfect parameter recovery. We propose a probabilistic causal framework for generating temporal graphs with transient edges and known ground-truth causal structure, allowing temporal link prediction to be evaluated jointly with causal parameter recovery. For the proposed binary logistic parametrisation, we derive the Cramér--Rao bound and validate the tradeoff between parameter estimation error and irreducible predictive loss. Our results show that predictive accuracy alone may not reflect whether a model has learned the underlying causal mechanism, motivating benchmarks that distinguish reducible model error from intrinsic process uncertainty.

</details>


### [18] [RANSAC Scoring Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27385)
*James Pritts,Felix Seegräber,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需内点尺度参数的RANSAC评分方法，通过共轭逆伽马先验解析边缘化内点尺度，在近7万图像对基准测试中超越了现有方法，尤其在阈值误标定和数据稀缺场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RANSAC变体需要用户提供依赖于内点尺度的阈值参数，而该尺度必须从污染数据中估计，这导致方法对参数敏感且适应性差。作者希望消除这一依赖，实现无需预设尺度的鲁棒模型拟合。

Method: 反转传统推理顺序：在固定内点划分条件下，利用共轭逆伽马先验对内点尺度进行解析边缘化，得到闭式评分函数。该评分统一了非信息性Jeffreys先验和信息性经验贝叶斯先验，并通过排序-扫描算法实现O(N log N)计算复杂度。

Result: 在近7万图像对上：1）阈值误标定时评分几乎保持平稳，基线方法显著下降；2）仅需2个验证对即可达到近最优精度，基线方法需约200个；3）验证数据稀疏时先验正则化自动收紧。

Conclusion: 该评分是首个真正无需内点尺度参数的RANSAC评分，在多种场景下超越现有方法，具有更强的鲁棒性和数据效率。

Abstract: The most widely used RANSAC variants score candidate models by counting inliers or summing per-point scores that saturate beyond a residual threshold. Every such score requires a user-supplied parameter that is a function of the inlier scale, which must itself be estimated from contaminated data. We remove this dependence by reversing the usual order of inference: rather than estimating the scale and then scoring against it, we marginalize the inlier scale analytically in closed form under a conjugate Inverse-Gamma prior for a fixed inlier partition, then optimize over partitions. A single closed-form expression spans the non-informative Jeffreys limit and informative empirical-Bayes priors, so the same score adapts across data-rich and data-scarce regimes without any change to the algorithm. The proposed RANSAC score is the first in which the inlier scale is genuinely absent from the formula. The score admits O(N log N ) computation via sort-and-sweep. On a benchmark of nearly 70 000 image pairs spanning different two-view estimation problems and both engineered and learned feature pipelines, the proposed score exceeds the state of the art (RANSAC, MSAC, GaU, MAGSAC): it stays nearly flat under threshold miscalibration where baselines degrade, reaches near-optimal accuracy from as few as two validation pairs where baselines need ont he order of 100 times more,. and tightens its prior regularization as validation data grows scarce.

</details>


### [19] [Unified Zero-Shot Time Series Forecasting: A Darts Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27438)
*Zhihao Dai,Dennis Bader,Alain Gysi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Darts库新增统一FoundationModel类，无缝集成多种时间序列基础模型（Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM），实现零样本/微调预测、不确定性估计和回测的全流程标准化接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型以独立包形式发布，接口碎片化且与通用工具链互操作性差，难以联合评估和集成到完整流程中。

Method: 在Darts中开发统一的FoundationModel类集合，为标准化的全周期预测接口提供最小外部依赖，使现有管道仅需更改名称即可使用基础模型。

Result: 实现了零样本或微调预测、不确定性估计、回测，以及与数据处理和评估工具的统一框架集成。

Conclusion: Darts通过统一接口降低了基础模型的使用门槛，推动了从训练自定义模型到使用预训练通用预测器的范式转换。

Abstract: Since its initial release in 2020, Darts has become a widely used open-source Python library for time series analysis. A series of foundation models have recently claimed accuracy improvements in zero-shot forecasting, promising a paradigm shift from training custom models to harnessing pre-trained general-purpose forecasters. Foundation models, however, are often released as isolated packages with fragmented interfaces and limited interoperability with common tooling, making joint evaluation and integration within complete pipelines difficult. In Darts, we developed a unified $\texttt{FoundationModel}$ class collection (Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, TiRex, PatchTST-FM) that provides standardized, full-cycle forecasting interfaces with minimal external dependencies for integrating foundation models into the ecosystem. Existing Darts pipelines can now use foundation models with only a name change; new pipelines can use them for zero-shot or fine-tuned forecasting, uncertainty estimation, and backtesting, combined with data processing and evaluation tooling, all within a unified framework.

</details>


### [20] [PairSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability from Pair Representations in Protein Co-Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27440)
*Giosue Migliorini,Aristofanis Rontogiannis,Grigori Guitchounts,Nicholas Franklin,Axel Elaldi,Olivia Viessmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairSAE提出了一种针对结构生物学基础模型中pairformer架构的稀疏自编码器方法，通过N-mode SVD压缩成对张量为token级交互角色，学习共享的token级特征并解码为序列和成对表示，解决了传统SAE在pairformer上的二次爆炸问题，在Boltz-2模型上实现了可解释的特征提取和亲和力预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏自编码器(SAE)在处理pairformer类架构时存在两个核心难题：直接操作成对表示会导致特征数量呈二次爆炸式增长，且序列表征和成对表征中分布的联合概念难以被有效捕捉。

Method: 通过N-mode SVD将成对张量压缩为token级别的交互角色，再使用稀疏自编码器学习一组共享的token级特征，这些特征能够解码为序列和成对两种表示，从而避免直接处理pairwise表示的组合爆炸问题。

Result: 在Boltz-2模型对PLINDER蛋白质-配体复合物的激活数据上，PairSAE学习到的特征与UniProt注释对齐，并能有效预测Boltz-2的亲和力值。

Conclusion: PairSAE成功将结构生物学基础模型的潜在空间与可解释的结构概念联系起来，阐明了模型"知道"什么，同时避免了传统SAE在pairformer架构上受到的限制。

Abstract: Foundation models for structural biology have achieved remarkable performance in predicting biomolecular structure and show promise for the design of proteins and small molecules. Yet understanding which internal features drive their outputs remains challenging. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs), effective on transformer-style sequence embeddings, do not transfer cleanly to pairformer-like architectures: naively operating on pairwise representations yields a quadratic blow-up of features and obscures concepts distributed jointly across sequence and pair representations.
  We introduce PairSAE, which summarizes pairwise tensors via an N-mode SVD into token-wise interaction roles, then uses a sparse autoencoder to learn a shared set of token-level features that decode into both sequence and pair representations. Evaluated on Boltz-2 activations for PLINDER protein-ligand complexes, PairSAE yields interpretable features that align with UniProt annotations and predict Boltz-2 affinity values. These results indicate that PairSAE links the latent space of foundation models for structural biology to interpretable structural concepts, clarifying what the model "knows" while avoiding pairformer-induced pitfalls that limit conventional SAEs.

</details>


### [21] [Learning in Markovian bandits with non-observable states and constrained decision epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27448)
*Thomas Hira,Victor Boone,Urtzi Ayesta,Ina Maria Verloop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非可观测状态且决策时刻可能受约束的马尔可夫强盗问题的遗憾最小化，提出自退化马尔可夫强盗模型，并设计UCB-NOM算法。结果表明，无先验知识时算法遗憾必为超对数阶（ω(log T)），但UCB-NOM可接近对数阶；有先验知识时可达O(log T)遗憾及O(√(T log T))最坏情况遗憾界，且界值与状态数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究马尔可夫强盗中状态不可观测且决策时刻受约束情况下的遗憾最小化问题，探索纯策略基准下的学习界限。

Method: 引入自退化马尔可夫强盗模型，设计基于乐观原则的UCB-NOM算法，利用bias函数先验知识进行参数设定。

Result: 无先验知识时遗憾必为ω(log T)；UCB-NOM实现近对数遗憾；有先验知识时达到O(log T)遗憾和O(√(T log T))最坏情况界。

Conclusion: 状态不可观测在自退化马尔可夫强盗中仅为轻微不便，遗憾界不依赖于状态数。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of regret minimization in Markovian bandits with \emph{non-observable states} and possibly \emph{constrained} decision epochs. The focus is restricted to a ``pure'' regret benchmark, that compares the performance of the learning algorithm to the best \emph{pure policy} which -- akin to optimal policies of stochastic bandits -- picks the optimal arm from start to finish without ever switching. We introduce a generalization of rested Markovian bandits, \emph{self-degrading Markovian bandits}, for which pure policies are always asymptotically optimal.We show that without prior knowledge on the underlying bandit, the regret of algorithms that switch arms rarely necessarily scales super-logarithmically for every bandit, i.e., as $ω(\log(T))$, where $T$ is the learning horizon. Despite the unreachability of the logarithmic regime, we design UCB-NOM, an optimistic algorithm inspired by UCB, of which the regret is nearly logarithmic. Lastly, we show that given prior knowledge on the Markovian bandit in the form of a bound on the bias functions of its arm, a proper instantiation of UCB-NOM achieves $O(\log(T))$ regret. We further show that this prior knowledge allows for a $O(\sqrt{T \log(T)})$ worst-case regret bound for UCB-NOM. Notably, our regret bounds do not depend on the number of states of the underlying Markov chains. Our findings suggest that the non-observability of states is a mild inconvenience in self-degrading Markovian bandits.

</details>


### [22] [Prism Transformer: Progressive Head Schedules for Hierarchical Attention Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27449)
*Shubham Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prism Transformer通过渐进式头部调度，在保持参数量和计算量不变的情况下，让模型浅层使用更少但更宽的注意力头捕捉复杂局部模式，深层使用更多但更窄的头学习专门化语言特征，从而在多个规模上优于传统均匀头分配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多头注意力在所有层均匀分配隐藏维度，导致浅层头部子空间维度受限，无法忠实捕获高维上下文模式，形成结构性瓶颈。

Method: 提出Prism Transformer架构，以渐进式头部调度替代静态均匀头配置：随层数增加单调增加头数，使浅层拥有更少但更宽的头（局部合成），深层拥有更多但更窄的头（解构为专门特征）。该设计参数中性、计算中性，零训练/推理开销。

Result: 在124M、354M、757M三个规模上，Prism Transformer一致优于均匀基线，验证损失降低，在PIQA、HellaSwag、ARC-Easy、WinoGrande等零样本基准上持续提升。

Conclusion: 非均匀子空间分配能够解锁标准Transformer预算内的潜在容量，实现模型能力的更有效利用。

Abstract: Multi-head attention conventionally partitions the hidden dimension equally across all heads at every layer, enforcing an identical representational subspace dimension (dh = dmodel/h) throughout the models depth. In this work, we identify this uniform allocation as a fundamental structural bottleneck: due to their restricted dimensional space, early-layer heads are unable to faithfully capture complex, high-dimensional contextual patterns. To resolve this, we introduce the Prism Transformer, a novel architectural paradigm that replaces the static, uniform head configuration with a progressive head schedule. By monotonically increasing the head count across layers, the Prism Transformer naturally establishes a local-to-global representational hierarchy: early layers leverage fewer, exceptionally wide heads to capture complex, local compositional patterns, while deep layers deploy many, narrow heads to decompose these patterns into specialized linguistic features. Crucially, this structural shift is parameter-neutral, compute-neutral, and introduces zero training or inference overhead, preserving identical weight matrices and FLOP budgets as the standard Transformer. Across three model scales (124M, 354M, and 757M), the Prism Transformer consistently outperforms uniform baselines, achieving consistent reductions in validation loss alongside consistent gains on downstream zero-shot benchmarks (including PIQA, HellaSwag, ARC-Easy, and WinoGrande). Our findings demonstrate that non-uniform subspace allocation unlocks latent capacity within the standard Transformer budget, enabling more effective use of model capacity.

</details>


### [23] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO）用于二维平环面上的三次非线性薛定谔方程，通过输入纵横比参数ω²来区分有理与无理几何结构，成功捕捉了不同几何下的Sobolev范数行为差异，并验证了几何条件化对长期预测精度的提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维平环面上的三次NLS方程中，纵横比的选择决定了傅里叶共振结构，有理数和无理数几何可能表现出不同的高频级联行为。现有数值方法难以高效捕捉这种几何依赖的动力学差异，因此需要一种几何感知的神经算子来学习谱转移现象。

Method: 设计了一种几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO），输入为解的实部与虚部以及纵横比参数ω²。模型通过监督学习训练以逼近一步解算子，使用随机相位初始数据和傅里叶伪谱法生成轨迹进行评估。

Result: 学习到的算子能够捕捉两种环面上的主要解动力学，再现了有理环面更强的H²增长和无理环面更受约束的行为，与文献结果一致。消融实验表明，包含ω²可提高长期预测精度，尤其对有理几何结构效果显著。

Conclusion: 几何条件化的神经算子能够有效学习非线性色散偏微分方程中的谱转移现象，支持在神经网络中显式引入几何参数以增强模型对结构敏感动力学的预测能力。

Abstract: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>


### [24] [The Curse of Multiple Mediators: Hidden Interaction Effects in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27510)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David Arbour,Aaron Mueller,Scott Niekum,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新推导了激活修补（Activation Patching）的因果估计量，发现其自然间接效应（NIE）包含组件间交互效应（INT），导致因果重要性评估产生偏差。INT无法通过调整估计量消除，且会掩盖或夸大组件重要性，但可作为诊断工具识别因果结论的提示依赖性和组合搜索的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补是机械可解释性中归因因果责任的主要工具，但其估计量NIE是否纯粹捕捉了单个组件的因果效应尚不明确，需要从因果中介分析角度重新审视其构成。

Method: 从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补的NIE估计量，识别出其中包含的交互效应（INT），通过GPT-2 IOI电路实验展示INT导致的失败模式，并理论分析INT的性质（缩放规律、局部仿射条件下的可忽略性、组合分解）。

Result: NIE包含INT成分，导致组件因果重要性被掩盖或夸大；INT解释了忠实度分数的不稳定性；INT随干净与修补激活距离缩放，局部仿射时可忽略，可分解为成对和高阶组交互。

Conclusion: INT无法消除，但可作为诊断工具：其大小和符号可提示因果结论对提示的依赖性，以及基于NIE的贪心排名会遗漏仅通过组合搜索才能发现的机制。

Abstract: Activation patching is the primary tool in mechanistic interpretability. It attributes causal responsibility for a model behavior to each of its individual components by estimating its natural indirect effect (NIE). Re-deriving the activation patching estimand from causal mediation analysis, we find that the NIE does not solely capture the causal effect through the specific component. It also contains interaction effects (INT) that measure how much the component's causal effect itself depends on the state of other components in the model. A natural response may be to try to eliminate INT by adjusting the estimator or unit of analysis, but each of these potential remedies has predictable failure modes. We demonstrate these failure modes in the GPT-2 IOI circuit; components whose causal importance is conditional on the state of other components are either invisible or artificially inflated, and INT variance explains the previously documented instability of faithfulness scores. We prove that INT scales with the distance between clean and patched component activations, is negligible when the model is locally affine, and decomposes combinatorially into pairwise and higher-order group interactions. Despite its inevitability, INT is not a nuisance to be eliminated, but rather a diagnostic for interpretability studies. Its individual and group-level magnitude and sign signal when causal conclusions are prompt-dependent, and when greedy NIE-based component ranking will miss mechanisms only discoverable through combinatorial search.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CO2地质封存中安全预测井底压力和 plume 迁移至关重要，但实际模拟常采用截断域，人为边界会扭曲压力扩散和CO2饱和度足迹，需要评估边界条件保真度对预测的影响。

Method: 对比十种简化域边界处理方法（包括均匀孔隙体积乘子、传导率修饰器、角修正孔隙体积校正、分层校正和渐变修饰器），以全域参考模拟为基准，在均质和非均质储层中使用BHP RMSE、NRMSE、峰值压力偏差和 plume 交并比（IoU）作为性能指标进行评估。

Result: 均匀处理忽略角存储导致BHP RMSE为362-382 psi（均质）和250-304 psi（非均质），IoU约0.80-0.84（16-20% plume区域误表）；角修正场景将压力误差大幅降低并将IoU提升至0.94以上；传导率修正并非普遍有益（均质中改善压力保真度，非均质中过度限制流动增加误差）；含传导率修正的渐变修饰器表现最一致，NRMSE<3.7%，IoU>0.97。

Conclusion: 截断域建模中角网格孔隙体积守恒是最重要的要求；传导率修正的效果依赖于储层均质性；含传导率修正的渐变修饰器是两类储层中最稳健的边界处理方法。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bottom-hole pressure (BHP) and CO2 plume migration is essential for safe geological carbon storage, yet practical simulations often rely on truncated domains where artificial boundaries distort pressure diffusion and CO2 saturation footprints. In this study, we evaluate how boundary-condition fidelity affects BHP and CO2 plume prediction by comparing ten reduced-domain boundary treatments against full-domain reference simulations in homogeneous and heterogeneous reservoirs. We test uniform pore-volume multipliers, transmissibility modifiers, corner-adjusted pore-volume corrections, layered corrections, and gradual modifiers using BHP RMSE, NRMSE, peak pressure deviation, and plume Intersection over Union (IoU) as performance metrics. Our results show that conserving corner pore volume is the most important requirement for truncated-domain modeling. We find that uniform treatments which neglect corner storage generate large pressure errors, with BHP RMSE of 362 to 382 psi in the homogeneous model and 250 to 304 psi in the heterogeneous model, and yield plume IoU values near 0.80 to 0.84, indicating roughly 16 to 20% of the combined plume area is misrepresented. Corner-adjusted scenarios substantially reduce pressure errors and raise plume IoU above 0.94, but we observe that transmissibility correction is not universally beneficial. In homogeneous reservoirs, uniform transmissibility adjustment improves pressure fidelity; in heterogeneous reservoirs, it can over-restrict flow across variable-permeability boundary faces, increasing BHP error and contracting the predicted plume. We find the gradual modifier with transmissibility correction provides the most consistent performance, achieving BHP NRMSE below 3.7% and plume IoU above 0.97 in both reservoir types.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种族和族裔等人口统计信号在法律和文化层面敏感，难以收集和使用。现有公平性度量依赖这些信号，但缺乏既能保护隐私又能进行细粒度公平性评估的方法。

Method: PPRE方法在两种种族/族裔信号源（BISG姓氏地理编码估计器和稀疏的自我报告人口统计黄金调查集）之上，叠加三种隐私技术：安全两方计算、差分隐私和加法同态加密，构建隐私保护的公平性度量解决方案。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可迁移的框架，供其他希望在机构内实施类似隐私保护度量基础设施的组织参考。

Abstract: Fairness measurements in the form of disaggregated evaluations often rely on demographic signals that are legally constrained or culturally sensitive. Race and ethnicity signals are among the more difficult signals to curate and use for this task. This paper presents Privacy-Preserving Probabilistic Race/Ethnicity Estimation (PPRE) as a method for enabling fairness measurements with respect to race/ethnicity for U.S.\ LinkedIn members in a privacy-preserving manner. PPRE applies privacy technologies (specifically: secure two-party computation, differential privacy, and additive homomorphic encryption) on top of two race/ethnicity demographic signal sources (the Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding estimator and a sparse golden survey set of self-reported demographics) to power a fairness measurement solution with respect to US-based race/ethnicity demographics. We detail its privacy guarantees and demonstrate its application on candidate- and viewer-side fairness measurements. We close with a transferable framework for institutions seeking to implement similar privacy-preserving measurement infrastructure.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典生成模型随规模扩大面临计算成本与效率挑战，量子机器学习提供了一种使用紧凑、高表达力模型表示复杂数据分布的有前景替代方案。

Method: 提出QDiffusion-TS框架，将经典扩散架构中的去噪Transformer前馈组件替换为量子神经网络，构建混合量子Transformer，实现参数量大幅缩减。

Result: 在苹果和亚马逊金融时间序列上，生成数据Wasserstein距离降低约44%；下游预测任务中数据增强使RMSE提升高达71%。

Conclusion: 量子增强架构能以更少参数一致匹配并常超越经典性能，为更高效可扩展的数据驱动生成建模建立了实用框架。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable success in data synthesis, though recent advances driven by increasing model scale have introduced challenges in computational cost and efficiency. Quantum machine learning offers a promising alternative, representing complex data distributions using compact, highly expressive models. Here, we propose QDiffusion-TS, the first quantum generative diffusion model for time series synthesis, and validate it on the IQM quantum processor. The framework extends a classical diffusion architecture by replacing feed-forward components within the denoising transformer with quantum neural networks, yielding a hybrid quantum transformer that reduces the number of trainable parameters in each replaced component by nearly three orders of magnitude. Evaluated on financial time series from Apple and Amazon, the model generates synthetic data that more accurately reproduces the real distributions, reducing Wasserstein distance by approximately 44% relative to its classical counterpart across both datasets. In a downstream forecasting task, augmentation with the generated data improves predictive performance by up to 71% in RMSE over a baseline trained solely on real data. These results show that quantum enhanced architectures can consistently match and frequently surpass classical performance with substantially fewer parameters, establishing a practical framework towards more efficient and scalable data-driven generative modelling.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高速公路风险评估方法缺乏对纵向（跟车）与横向（换道）交互的区分建模，且图结构模型在横向冲突风险上的优势未被系统验证，亟需一种既能捕获关系结构、又能提供可解释归因的帧级风险评估方法。

Method: 将帧级风险评估建模为多智能体场景图级二分类问题；构建关系感知图，以车辆为节点、同车道和邻车道为两类边，并增强物理信息边特征；提出HIA-GAT，一种双流异构图注意力网络，通过专用注意力路径分别处理纵向和横向交互，采用冲突类型感知门控机制融合，门控由SSM冲突归因提供事件级监督。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集上，基于九种TTC和PET阈值配置，HIA-GAT取得最佳平均风险排序性能（AUC 0.835/0.867）；在仅使用PET（横向冲突）的设置中增益最大；图结构对横向冲突风险建模至关重要，纵向风险可通过非关系聚合捕获。

Conclusion: HIA-GAT在帧级高速公路风险评估任务上优于非图模型和图基线方法；门控机制提供可解释的每车辆主导冲突类型归因，支持实时、可操作的高速公路安全监控。

Abstract: This paper formulates frame-level freeway risk assessment as a multi-agent scene graph-level binary classification problem, where each video or trajectory frame is labeled risky if any TTC- or PET-based conflict violates a specified severity threshold. We construct a relation-aware graph per frame with vehicles as nodes and two interaction types as edges: same-lane (longitudinal) and adjacent-lane (lateral), augmented with physics-informed edge features aligned to rear-end and lane-change conflict mechanisms. Building on a structured benchmarking suite of non-graph models and graph baselines, we propose HIA-GAT, a dual-stream heterogeneous graph attention network that processes longitudinal and lateral interactions through dedicated attention pathways and fuses them via a conflict-type-aware gating mechanism with event-level gate supervision derived from SSM conflict attribution. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 freeway datasets across nine TTC and PET threshold configurations show that HIA-GAT achieves the best average risk-ranking performance (AUC 0.835 on I-80 and 0.867 on US-101), with the largest gains on PET-only (lane-change) settings where relational structure is essential. Beyond accuracy, the learned gate provides interpretable per-vehicle attribution of dominant conflict type, supporting actionable, real-time freeway safety monitoring. We show that graph structure is critical for modeling lateral conflict risk, while longitudinal risk can often be captured by non-relational aggregation.

</details>


### [29] [PEBS: Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage for RLHF Reward-Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27578)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEBS是一种针对RLHF奖励模型中的每个标注者进行经验贝叶斯收缩估计的后验方法，通过拟合每个标注者的仿射校准器并应用James-Stein经验贝叶斯收缩，在不重新训练奖励模型的情况下，在PRISM和PluriHarms数据集上分别实现了8.58%和9.66%的RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF奖励模型将所有标注者的偏好进行池化，拟合一个全局的仿射校准器，忽略了不同标注者之间系统性的评分尺度偏移和斜率差异，导致平均拟合结果无法代表任何单个标注者。

Method: PEBS为每个标注者拟合独立的仿射校准器，并在保留的标注数据上计算，然后应用Morris-James-Stein经验贝叶斯收缩向总体均值收缩，整个过程以闭式形式完成，无需重新训练奖励模型。

Result: 在PRISM数据集上，PEBS相比池化总体斜率基线将用户内保留集RMSE降低了8.58%；在PluriHarms危害评分数据上实现了9.66%的RMSE降低。

Conclusion: PEBS是一种高效的闭式后验估计器，能够在保持奖励基础模型不变的情况下，仅估计推理时使用的标注者级映射，实现个性化的仿射校准。

Abstract: Reward models for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pool preferences across thousands of annotators and fit one global affine calibrator, collapsing raters with systematically different rating-scale offsets and slopes into a single average-rater fit that does not match any individual annotator. PEBS is a per-rater empirical-Bayes shrinkage estimator: it fits per-rater affine calibrators on a held-out slice of each annotator's ratings and applies Morris-James-Stein empirical-Bayes shrinkage toward the population mean, in closed form and without retraining the reward model. On PRISM, PEBS reduces within-user held-out RMSE by 8.58% over the pooled population-slope baseline. The procedure replicates on PluriHarms harm ratings (Qwen-2.5 base, in-family) with a +9.66% RMSE reduction over the same population-slope baseline. PEBS is a closed-form post-hoc estimator for annotator-specific affine calibration in RLHF reward modeling; it leaves the reward base model unchanged and estimates only the rater-level map used at inference time for new ratings.

</details>


### [30] [Retroactive Advantage Correction: Closed-Form V-Trace Bias Correction for Delay-Aware RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27580)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**回溯优势修正（RAC）**，解决RLHF中异步奖励信号（如代码执行验证器、慢法官集成、排队人工审核）破坏PPO同步奖励假设的问题。RAC将未完成的慢速反馈排队、通过非负核函数老化、并作为裁剪残差注入下一优化步的优势计算中。实验证明RAC在无偏重要性比率下累积修正无偏，在双慢通道配置下将策略偏差降低最高47.9倍，且仅需两行代码即可集成到PPO/GRPO中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RLHF并不总是具备同步奖励信号：代码执行验证器、慢速法官集成和排队人工审核等场景下，奖励信号可能滞后若干梯度步才返回，破坏了标准PPO所依赖的同步奖励假设，导致策略更新出现偏差。

Method: 提出回溯优势修正（RAC）：将每个未完成的慢速反馈结果排队，通过非负核函数进行老化处理，并以裁剪残差的形式注入下一优化步的优势计算中。该方法在性质上证明了当有效延迟核完全注入所有质量时，累积RAC修正是无偏的；在未注入部分则携带线性偏差；在无延迟的恒等核下退化为V-trace。

Result: 在表格型MDP概念验证中，RAC在双慢通道配置下将闭式策略偏差降低最高47.9倍，且比"等待慢反馈"策略具有更低的墙钟成本。

Conclusion: RAC有效解决了异步奖励信号下的策略优化偏差问题，与PPO和GRPO兼容，仅需两行奖励管理器补丁即可集成，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in production does not always have a synchronous reward signal. Code-execution verifiers, slow judge ensembles, and queued human review can return several gradient steps after the rollout that produced them, breaking the synchronous-reward assumption underlying standard PPO. We address this gap with Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC): each pending slow completion is queued, aged through a non-negative kernel, and reinjected as a clipped residual into the next optimiser step's advantage. We prove that under an unbiased clipped importance ratio, the cumulative RAC correction is exactly unbiased when the effective delay kernel reinjects all of its mass, and carries a bias linear in the unreinjected fraction otherwise; at the no-delay identity kernel it reduces to V-trace. On a tabular Markov decision process (MDP) proof-of-concept, RAC reduces the closed-form policy bias by up to 47.9x at the two-slow-channel configuration, beating wait-for-slow at lower wall-clock cost. RAC integrates with PPO and GRPO through a two-line reward-manager patch.

</details>


### [31] [Global Explanations for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Models via $K$-Order Markov Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27599)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KARMA方法，通过构建马尔可夫代理模型来解释时间序列预测器，捕捉时间依赖性，并恢复数据因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法大多假设时间戳特征独立，忽略了时间序列预测中时间依赖性和因果结构这一根本属性，导致解释违反数据顺序结构。

Method: KARMA方法包含三方面：1) 识别预测充分的最小历史长度K；2) 从离散化历史空间估计最优K阶马尔可夫转移核；3) 从转移核导出五级全局解释层次结构。

Result: 在真实天气数据（北京PM2.5）上展示了五级解释层次结构；在合成数据上验证了KARMA能恢复模型学习到的因果结构，且比TimeSHAP等归因方法更好地识别时间依赖性。

Conclusion: KARMA通过马尔可夫代理模型有效解释时间序列预测器，捕捉时序依赖关系，在因果结构恢复和时间依赖性识别上优于现有归因方法。

Abstract: While many explainable AI (XAI) methods have been proposed, most are not designed for time-series forecasting models and often rely on the implicit assumption that timestamp features are independent. This assumption ignores the fundamental property of temporal dependence and can lead to explanations that violate the sequential and causal structure of the data. We introduce \textsc{KARMA}, a method for explaining time-series predictors by constructing a Markov surrogate model that captures the temporal dependencies learned by the predictor. Our approach revolves around three main aspects: identifying the minimal history length $K$ that is predictively sufficient for the model, estimating the best-fitting $K$-order Markov transition kernel from the discretized history space, and a five-level global explanation hierarchy that can be derived from the Markov transition kernel, which we illustrate using real-world weather data (Beijing PM 2.5). We also certify using complex synthetic data with known true causal edges that KARMA (i) recovers the data causal structure as learned by the model via a controlled experiment and (ii) identifies temporal dependencies better than established attribution methods such as TimeSHAP.

</details>


### [32] [Halt Fast! Early Stopping for Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27694)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种元学习框架，通过轻量级元预测器生成图像特定的序贯E-Process先验，实现自适应计算资源分配的随时有效鲁棒性认证，在保持严格统计保证的同时将样本复杂度降低20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑（RS）为神经网络提供了严格的鲁棒性保证，但极端的计算成本（每次输入需数万次模型评估）和固定的先验样本量限制限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一种元学习框架，利用轻量级元学习器预测图像特定的先验，用于序贯E-Process，实现随时有效的认证（anytime-valid certification），自适应分配计算资源。

Result: 相比传统方法，样本复杂度降低20倍；实现基于应用特定风险阈值的自适应计算分配，同时保持与传统方法相当的认证性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为实时、安全关键的认证部署提供了可行路径，实现了经典认证框架下不可能的"资源分诊"（resource triage）能力。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) provides rigorous robustness guarantees for neural networks without architectural constraints, yet its adoption is limited by extreme computational costs. Standard RS requires tens of thousands of model evaluations per input and forces practitioners to commit to fixed sample sizes a priori. In this work, we present a novel meta-learning framework for anytime-valid certified robustness that adaptively deploys computational resources. By using a lightweight meta-learner to predict image-specific priors for a sequential E-process, we achieve a 20-fold reduction in sample complexity compared to traditional methods while maintaining rigorous statistical guarantees. Beyond raw efficiency, we demonstrate how anytime-validity enables adaptively allocating compute based upon application-specific risk thresholds, a form of resource triage impossible under classic certification frameworks. That this is achievable while also providing similar certification performance demonstrates that our approach provides a pathway for real-time, safety-critical certification deployments.

</details>


### [33] [COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27611)
*Chuanhao Li,Xiaoan Xu,Dirk Bergemann,Ethan X. Fang,Yehua Wei,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COOPA是一个模块化LLM智能体架构，通过迭代置信度建模、元素级来源追踪和多求解器路由，在运筹学建模任务中实现了优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的运筹学建模系统存在复杂问题准确率低、输出不透明、求解器支持范围狭窄三大局限，需要更可靠、可解释且可扩展的决策支持方案。

Method: COOPA包含三个核心组件：(1) 迭代置信度建模——生成多个候选公式化描述并自评估，通过最大-最小置信度准则选择最优解；(2) 元素级来源追踪与置信度解释——将变量、参数、约束和目标与源文本关联，提供可审计线索；(3) 多求解器路由——将问题路由至专门优化智能体。

Result: 在三个OR基准、八个LLM主干和四个基线下的对比中，COOPA在六个主干上取得最佳宏平均准确率，相比最强基线提升最高6.7个百分点。

Conclusion: COOPA通过模块化设计和可解释性机制，有效提升了LLM在运筹学建模任务中的准确性和可靠性，其中迭代置信度建模、来源可追溯性和多求解器调度均被验证具有实际价值。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) provides a rigorous framework for high-stakes decision-making, but effective OR modeling requires substantial domain knowledge, mathematical abstraction, and solver expertise. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet remain limited by low accuracy on complex problems, opaque outputs, and narrow solver support. We propose COOPA (COoperative OPerations Agent), a modular LLM-agent architecture for interpretable and scalable OR decision support. It combines three components: iterative confidence-based modeling, which generates multiple candidate formulations, self-evaluates them across modeling dimensions, and selects one using a max-min confidence criterion; element-level provenance and confidence explanations, which link variables, parameters, constraints, and objectives to quoted source text and provide an audit trail for human verification; and multi-solver routing to specialized optimizer agents for different OR problem classes. Across three OR benchmarks, eight LLM backbones, and four baselines under identical conditions, COOPA achieves the best macro-average accuracy on six of eight backbones and improves over the strongest baseline by up to 6.7 percentage points. A within-system ablation isolates the contribution of iterative confidence-based modeling, while additional analyses and case studies illustrate the value of source traceability and multi-solver dispatch.

</details>


### [34] [What Was That Again? Certified Robustness for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27698)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil Marchant,Jiani Xie,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于认证机制的双门诊断流程（双面原子审计+排名锦标赛），在无需真值标签的情况下，显著降低ASR系统的词错误率（最高55%），提高召回率，并降低置信度与WER的斯皮尔曼相关性，同时提供词级和句级认证以增强声学安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统对对抗性和良性扰动高度敏感，但在实际部署中由于缺乏真实转录（oracle knowledge）而难以检测此类行为。

Method: 提出双门诊断流程：(1) 双面原子审计，通过积累统计财富（statistical wealth）来认证token的存在性和对抗性排除；(2) 排名锦标赛，选择获胜序列。

Result: 在四种不同架构上的评估表明，WER相对降低高达55%，同时召回率提高，置信度与WER的斯皮尔曼相关性降低，并提供细粒度的词级和句级认证。

Conclusion: 受认证启发（certification-inspired）的机制能够在无真值标签条件下有效提升ASR系统的鲁棒性和声学安全性。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition systems are notoriously both sensitive to adversarial and benign perturbations. While this has been repeatedly demonstrated using reference datasets, detecting such behaviors in deployed systems is incredibly challenging, due to the absence of oracle knowledge of the true transcription. We demonstrate that employing a certification-inspired mechanism can significantly decrease WER, increase recall, and decrease the Spearman correlation between confidence and WER. We achieve this through a dual-gate diagnostic pipeline: a Two-Sided Atomic Audit that accumulates statistical wealth to certify both token existence and adversarial exclusion, and a Rank-Based Tournament that selects the winning sequence. Our evaluations across four diverse architectures demonstrate up to a 55% relative reduction in Word Error Rate, while also providing granular word- and sentence-level certifications to enhance acoustic security.

</details>


### [35] [FoggyTrust: Robust Federated Learning with Hierarchical Trust Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27622)
*Emmanuel Rassou,Tomas Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOGGYTRUST 提出了一种分层信任联邦学习框架，通过将信任计算本地化到雾节点，在保持鲁棒性的同时更好地处理全局异构数据，在CIFAR-10上比FLTrust提升超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法（如FLTrust）在全局数据异构场景下，信任估计存在分布不匹配问题，且无法有效处理客户端漂移。

Method: 提出FOGGYTRUST，将FLTrust扩展为分层架构：雾节点负责局部信任计算（处理同构客户端组），全局服务器结合异构感知优化器（如FedAdam、SCAFFOLD）进行聚合，同时解决信任估计偏差和组间客户端漂移。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，面对Krum和Trim攻击时，FOGGYTRUST相比FLTrust提升超过50%；在真实野生动物监测数据集上验证了分层信任网络的有效性。

Conclusion: 分层信任架构能有效解决联邦学习中的数据异构性和鲁棒性冲突，在高风险安全关键场景（如野生动物监测）中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust federated learning seeks to protect distributed model training from malicious or corrupted clients without requiring access to their private data. FLTrust addresses this challenge by introducing a trusted server-side root dataset that assigns trust scores to client updates for more robust aggregation. In this work, we propose FOGGYTRUST, a hierarchical extension of FLTrust that localizes trust computation to fog nodes, allowing the framework to better handle globally heterogeneous data while preserving robustness within locally homogeneous client groups. We further show that this two-level architecture can simultaneously address distribution mismatch in trust estimation and client drift across groups by combining local trust-based aggregation with heterogeneity-aware global optimizers such as FedAdam and SCAFFOLD. Across benchmark datasets, FOGGYTRUST achieves its strongest gains on more challenging heterogeneous settings, particularly on CIFAR-10 under Krum and Trim attacks, where it achieves an over 50% improvement over FLTrust. We also test FOGGYTRUST in a real-world safari dataset to show the promise of hierarchical trust networks for robust federated learning in socially impactful, safety-critical settings such as distributed wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [36] [HybridCodec: Modeling Discrete and Continuous Representations for Efficient Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27627)
*Artem Ploujnikov,Francesco Verdini,Samir Sadok,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合离散标记和连续残差的混合编解码框架，在保持自回归效率的同时改善说话人特征保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散音频表示在多模态系统和大语言模型中广泛使用，但离散化过程导致信息丢失，降低了各类下游任务的性能。

Method: 提出混合离散-连续焦点调制编解码器（hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec）和混合Transformer架构，在离散域进行自回归推理，同时结合非自回归预测和连续残差上采样。

Result: 相比纯离散方法，显著改善了说话人特征的保留，同时减少了所需的自回归步数。

Conclusion: 混合离散-连续策略能有效缓解离散化信息损失问题，在保持效率的同时提升下游任务表现。

Abstract: Discrete audio representations have become increasingly popular for building multimodal text-audio systems and integrating audio capabilities into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, numerous studies report performance degradation on various downstream tasks due to information loss during discretization. To address this, we propose a novel approach combining temporally compressed discrete tokens with dimensionality-reduced continuous residuals. Our framework consists of a hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec and a hybrid Transformer. This architecture performs autoregressive inference in the discrete domain, coupled with non-autoregressive prediction and continuous residual upsampling. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the retention of speaker characteristics compared to discrete-only methods, while simultaneously reducing the number of required autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [37] [Continual Learning for Sequential Personalization of Small Language Models: A Stability Monitoring Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27634)
*Thomas S. Paula,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了小语言模型（SLM）在边缘设备上进行顺序LoRA个性化时的灾难性遗忘问题，提出通过检查点级协议监控任务性能、遗忘情况和参考集漂移，并展示轻量级参考集分布诊断可以揭示模型特定的不稳定模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM越来越多地部署在边缘设备上用于个性化应用，但个性化需要模型持续适应用户或任务特定的数据流，这属于持续学习场景，存在灾难性遗忘的风险。现有基准如TRACE已表明持续微调会显著降低对齐大语言模型的通用能力，因此需要研究SLM在持续个性化过程中的稳定性。

Method: 采用顺序LoRA个性化方法，在每个适应阶段后保存模型检查点，并在当前任务、先前任务和固定参考集上进行评估。通过这种检查点级协议监控任务性能、遗忘程度和参考集漂移，并利用轻量级参考集分布诊断来识别模型特定的不稳定模式。

Result: 轻量级参考集分布诊断能够揭示顺序LoRA个性化过程中SLM的特定不稳定模式，包括那些仅靠任务级指标难以发现的潜在有害适应情况。

Conclusion: 本文提出的监控方法有助于发现SLM在持续学习场景中的稳定性问题，为未来的研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly being considered for deployment on edge devices such as laptops, enabling private, low-latency, and locally personalized applications. However, personalization requires models to adapt over time to evolving user- or task-specific data, placing them in a continual learning setting. This creates the risk of catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously learned tasks or broader model capabilities. Recent benchmarks such as TRACE have shown that continual fine-tuning can significantly degrade the general abilities of aligned large language models. In this work, we present a study for sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs. We save model checkpoints after each adaptation stage and evaluate them on current tasks, previously seen tasks, and a fixed reference set. This checkpoint-level protocol enables us to monitor task performance, forgetting, and reference set drift over time.
