Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADC-GNN（注意力引导的扩散-对比图神经网络），通过扩散引导的特征增强、对比表示学习和多跳光谱注意力机制，解决图欺诈检测中的标签稀疏/不平衡和表征稀释问题，在1%训练比例下优于多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实欺诈图存在两个耦合挑战：一是稀疏且不平衡的监督（欺诈标签稀少且严重偏向正常账户），二是表征稀释（空间消息传递可能过度平滑伪装异常，光谱滤波器可能抑制欺诈相关的中高频不规则性）。

Method: ADC-GNN包含三个核心模块：1) 扩散引导的特征增强模块（构建噪声扰动节点特征视图，使用余弦调度进行特征空间去噪增强，而非全拓扑生成式图扩散）；2) 对比表示学习（稳定不同扰动下的节点表示）；3) 多跳光谱注意力模块（自适应强调与欺诈相关的跳级和关系级线索）。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个专有电信交易数据集（约6万条记录）上评估，1%训练设置下ADC-GNN在公开基准上一致优于原始图欺诈基线和四个近期图异常/欺诈基线。额外分析了分割稳定性、训练比例、过采样方案、模块消融、扩散调度以及运行时和内存消耗。

Conclusion: ADC-GNN统一框架通过扩散引导的特征增强、对比学习和多跳光谱注意力有效解决了图欺诈检测中的监督稀疏和表征稀释问题，在极低训练比例下获得一致性能提升。

Abstract: Graph-based fraud detection is essential for safeguarding large-scale transaction systems, where undetected anomalies may lead to substantial financial losses and security risks. Real-world fraud graphs pose two coupled challenges: sparse and imbalanced supervision, where verified fraudulent labels are scarce and heavily skewed toward benign accounts, and representation dilution, where spatial message passing may oversmooth camouflaged anomalies while spectral filters may suppress fraud-relevant mid- and high-frequency irregularities. To address these challenges, we propose ADC-GNN, short for Attention-guided Diffusion-Contrastive Graph Neural Network, a unified framework that combines diffusion-guided feature augmentation, contrastive representation learning, and multi-hop spectral attention for few-shot graph fraud detection. The diffusion component is formulated as a feature-space denoising augmentation mechanism rather than a full topology-generative graph diffusion model: it constructs noise-perturbed node-feature views under a cosine schedule and uses contrastive learning to stabilize node representations across perturbations. The spectral attention module further adaptively emphasizes fraud-relevant hop-level and relation-level cues. We evaluate ADC-GNN primarily on three public benchmarks and additionally report a proprietary real-world telecom transaction dataset with approximately 60,000 records as a private case study. Under the 1% training setting, ADC-GNN achieves consistent improvements over original graph fraud baselines and four protocol-consistent recent graph anomaly/fraud baselines on the public benchmarks. Additional analyses on split stability, training ratios, oversampling alternatives, module-level ablations, diffusion schedules, and runtime and memory-consumption comparisons further characterize the effective operating regime of ADC-GNN.

</details>


### [76] [MixTTA: Low-Rank Cross-Channel Mixing for Reliable Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28142)
*Mansoo Jung,Youngwook Kim,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MixTTA通过在归一化层中引入低秩跨通道变换模块，解决了传统TTA方法仅调整仿射参数（逐通道缩放和平移）无法处理分布偏移导致的跨通道结构变化的问题。该方法包含解耦投影和谱投影技术，可无缝集成到现有基于归一化的TTA方法中，在标准与wild TTA设置下均取得一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法通常仅更新归一化层的逐通道仿射参数，这种轴对齐的缩放和平移在几何上无法纠正分布偏移引起的跨通道结构变化，导致适应能力受限。

Method: 提出MixTTA轻量级插件模块：(1) 在归一化层中引入低秩跨通道变换，实现层间通道混合；(2) 解耦投影（Decoupling Projection）确保低秩分支与对角仿射路径严格分离；(3) 谱投影（Spectral Projection）防止在非平稳测试流下发生秩-1坍塌。

Result: 在标准TTA和wild TTA两种设置下均取得一致性改进，优于现有强基线方法，并在具有挑战性的条件下缓解了适应失败问题。

Conclusion: MixTTA作为一种即插即用的轻量模块，有效弥补了现有归一化基TTA方法在跨通道建模能力上的不足，提升了模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods commonly update the affine parameters of normalization layers to adapt deployed models under distribution shifts. However, per-channel affine parameters perform axis-aligned scaling and shifting, making them geometrically incapable of correcting cross-channel structural changes induced by distribution shift. To address this limitation, we propose MixTTA, a lightweight plug-in module that equips normalization layers with a low-rank cross-channel transformation, enabling inter-channel mixing at each layer. To ensure that the low-rank branch captures only cross-channel interactions, we also propose Decoupling Projection that enforces strict separation from the diagonal affine path, along with Spectral Projection that prevents rank-1 collapse under non-stationary test streams. MixTTA can be seamlessly integrated into any existing normalization-based TTA method. Experiments in both standard and wild TTA settings show consistent improvements over strong baselines while mitigating adaptation failure under challenging conditions. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/delta6189/MixTTA.

</details>


### [77] [Autoencoder Architectures for Athlete Performance Scoring from Wearable Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28145)
*Mateusz Kubita,Jan Zubalewicz,Krzysztof Siwek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种降维模型（三种自编码器变体、PCA和变分自编码器）在压缩九维跑步者传感器数据为单维潜得分方面的性能，发现深度自编码器在重构误差和综合评分上最优，而跑步配速、有氧解偶联和平均心率是潜得分的核心驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备产生大量高维训练日志，数据解释而非数据收集已成为瓶颈，亟需一种能在无监督条件下将多传感器跑步者数据压缩为可解释的单维性能指标的方法。

Method: 使用五种降维模型（三种自编码器变体、PCA和VAE）将九维传感器数据压缩为单维潜得分；采用重构误差（MSE）和潜得分可解释性（Spearman秩相关、Kendall秩相关、互信息、置换重要性）两个互补指标评估模型；通过组合选择标准防止仅依赖重构精度；使用改进的Borda计数聚合特征排名，并通过Bootstrap验证稳定性；包含两特征线性基线作为对比。

Result: 深度自编码器取得最低重构误差和最高综合评分；拓宽PCA隐藏层后，深层变体与深度自编码器在综合评分上接近，表明限制因素是隐藏层容量而非一维瓶颈；跑步配速、有氧解偶联和平均心率在所有模型和重采样中均为潜得分的主导驱动因素，与已知生理学一致。

Conclusion: 深度自编码器是无监督跑步者性能指标压缩的最优选择；隐藏层容量比瓶颈维度更关键；潜得分的核心特征具有生理学合理性，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Wearable devices produce large, high dimensional training logs for everyday runners, and interpretation rather than data collection is now the limiting step. This paper evaluates five dimensionality reduction models, three autoencoder variants, PCA, and a Variational Autoencoder, on their ability to compress nine sensor runner profiles into a single scalar performance indicator, the latent score. Because the setting is fully unsupervised, model quality is assessed along two complementary axes: reconstruction error (Mean Squared Error) and latent score interpretability, measured via Spearman and Kendall rank correlations, Mutual Information, and Permutation Importance. These are combined into a composite selection criterion that prevents selecting models on reconstruction accuracy alone. Feature rankings from the four metrics are aggregated via a modified Borda count, and their stability is confirmed by bootstrap validation. A two feature linear baseline is included to anchor the comparison. Deep autoencoder achieved the lowest reconstruction error and the highest composite score. Once the PCA hidden layers were widened, the deeper variants became closely competitive with Deep AE on the composite criterion, indicating that the limiting factor was hidden layer capacity rather than the one dimensional bottleneck. Running pace, aerobic decoupling, and average heart rate emerged as the dominant latent score drivers across all models and resampling runs, consistent with established physiology.

</details>


### [78] [Recovering Sharp Conductivity Features in the Finite-Data Calderón Problem with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28158)
*Ali AlHadi Kalout,Pablo Tejerina-Pérez,Konstantin Karchev,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez,Leonid Sarieddine,Raul Jimenez,Max Engelstein,Guy David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的Calderón逆问题求解框架，通过多尺度小波边界激励和傅里叶特征编码（FFE）来重建电导率分布。实验表明，该方法能从有限边界测量数据中以约3%-12%的相对误差恢复主要电导率结构，FFE有助于重建尖锐特征但并非普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从有限边界数据出发的Calderón逆问题（电阻抗成像），克服传统数值方法对大量边界数据的依赖，提升神经网络框架对尖锐电导率变化的重建能力。

Method: 1) 采用多尺度随机小波函数生成边界激励；2) 用两个独立的神经网络分别表示未知电导率和依赖于边界激励的电势族；3) 物理信息残差强制执行椭圆PDE；4) 通过边界损失融入有限Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN)数据；5) 引入傅里叶特征编码（FFE）增强高频信息表达。

Result: 在包含夹杂物、尖锐界面、平滑轮廓和异质介质的合成数据上，相对重建误差约为3%-12%；FFE改善了对局部尖锐特征（夹杂物和界面）的重建，但原始坐标网络在平滑场上表现更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 坐标表示方式和边界激励设计是神经Calderón反演中的关键因素，FFE并非在所有场景下都最优，需根据电导率场的特性进行选择。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have recently emerged as a promising framework for addressing the Calderón inverse problem from limited boundary data. In this work, we revisit neural Calderón inversion by introducing multiscale boundary excitations based on randomized wavelet functions and investigating the role of Fourier-feature encoding (FFE) for representing sharp conductivity variations. We propose a physics-informed reconstruction framework that represents the unknown conductivity and the associated family of electric potentials with separate neural networks conditioned on the applied boundary excitations. The governing elliptic PDE is enforced through physics-informed residuals, while finite Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) data are incorporated through boundary losses. Using synthetic data from a finite-difference forward solver, we evaluate the method on conductivity fields with inclusions, sharp interfaces, smooth profiles, and heterogeneous media. Results show that the framework recovers dominant conductivity structures from finite boundary measurements with relative errors between $3\%-12\%$ approximately. We show that FFE improves the reconstruction of localized sharp features, particularly for inclusions and interfaces, but are not universally optimal, with raw-coordinate networks performing competitively for smoother fields. These results highlight coordinate representations and boundary excitation design as key factors in neural Calderón inversion.

</details>


### [79] [CPAgents: Agentic Composite Phenotype Generation for Cardiac Disease Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28179)
*Zuoou Li,Wenlong Zhao,Kelly Yu,Weitong Zhang,Paul M. Matthews,Wenjia Bai,Bernhard Kainz,Mengyun Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CPAgents框架，通过三个智能体（分析器、提议器、验证器）自动构建可解释的复合影像表型，在人口规模心脏影像队列中显著提升了疾病判别性能（72个分类器-疾病-指标组合中56个排名第一）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表型组关联研究依赖预定义的单变量表型或专家手工特征，无法捕捉临床有意义的非线性效应和跨表型交互作用，限制了疾病风险分层能力。

Method: 提出CPAgents框架，包含三个协作智能体：分析器识别统计病理并提出候选变换；提议器在数值安全规则下生成受约束的医学与统计驱动的表达式；验证器通过多阶段标准评估候选表型并生成透明证据链。

Result: 在人口规模心脏影像队列中，发现的复合表型显著提升疾病判别能力：72个分类器-疾病-指标组合中56个排名第一（对比基线仅18个），所有九类临床疾病均观察到增益。

Conclusion: 该框架生成紧凑、临床可解释的表型公式及透明证据链，实现了超越专家驱动特征选择的更强表型-疾病关联的可拓展发现。

Abstract: Identifying robust associations between cardiac imaging phenotypes and clinical diseases is fundamental to population-scale cardiovascular research and reliable risk stratification. However, current phenome-wide association studies rely on pre-defined, single-variable phenotypes or expert-crafted features, which limits their ability to capture clinically meaningful non-linear effects and cross-phenotype interactions. To address this, we propose CPAgents, an iterative phenotype-Composition framework for cardiovascular Phenome-wide association study (PheWAS) that automatically constructs and validates interpretable composite phenotypes (e.g., polynomial, ratio, and interaction forms) from base imaging features. Specifically, our system coordinates three agents: (i) an Analyst that identifies statistical pathologies and nominates candidate transformations; (ii) a Proposer that generates constrained, medically and statistically motivated expressions under numerical safety rules; and (iii) a Verifier that evaluates candidates using multi-stage criteria and produces transparent evidence trails for accepted phenotypes. Evaluated on a population-scale cardiac imaging cohort, the discovered composite phenotypes markedly improve disease discrimination: across 72 classifier-disease-metric combinations, our variants achieve the top rank in 56 cases versus 18 for baselines, with gains observed across all nine clinical disease categories. Our framework yields compact, clinically interpretable phenotype formulas with transparent evidence trails, enabling scalable discovery of stronger phenotype-disease associations beyond expert-driven feature selection.

</details>


### [80] [The Remittance Blueprint: Data-driven Intelligence for Sri Lanka](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28190)
*Dhinanjaya Fernando,Dinura Ginige,Kalana Lakshan,Chanupa Gurusinghe,Lasana Pahanga,Subavarshana Arumugam,Sandeepa Weerasekara,Sandareka Wickramanayake,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于32年（1994-2025）月度数据，运用时间序列模型和机器学习方法分析斯里兰卡移民汇款，发现汇率和油价是主要驱动因素，Ridge回归比SARIMA提升73.8%精度，预测2026年汇款约90.01亿美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究斯里兰卡长达32年间的移民汇款驱动因素，揭示其与国内外经济变量的结构性依赖关系，为政策制定提供实证依据。

Method: 构建384个月度标准化数据集，采用探索性数据分析、平稳性修正的时间序列建模（ADF检验、Johansen协整、VAR/VECM）以及监督学习（包括Ridge回归、SARIMA）进行对比分析。

Result: 汇款流入主要由外部宏观变量（汇率动态和全球油价）驱动而非国内指标；脉冲响应确认汇率贬值和油价冲击的非对称影响；多变量机器学习模型优于传统单变量方法，Ridge回归相比SARIMA实现73.8%的精度提升（年化RMSE为4.948亿美元），在稳定条件下预测2026年汇款为90.01亿美元。

Conclusion: 斯里兰卡汇款对全球经济存在结构性依赖，需通过稳健的汇率政策、技术移民和正规金融渠道增强长期经济韧性。

Abstract: This study analyzes Sri Lankan migration and remittances over 32 years (1994-2025). Using a 384-month harmonized dataset, we apply exploratory data analysis, stationarity corrected time-series modeling (ADF, Johansen, VAR/VECM), and supervised learning. Results reveal remittance inflows are primarily driven by external macroeconomic variables, specifically exchange rate dynamics and global oil prices, rather than domestic indicators. Impulse response analysis confirms the asymmetric impact of currency depreciation and oil price shocks. Predictively, multivariate machine learning models outperform traditional univariate approaches; Ridge Regression achieves a 73.8% accuracy improvement over SARIMA (Annualized RMSE: USD 494.8 Mn). The optimized framework projects 2026 remittances at USD 9,001 million under stable conditions. These findings highlight the structural dependence of remittances on global economies, emphasizing the need for robust exchange rate policies, skilled migration, and formal financial channels to enhance long-term economic resilience.

</details>


### [81] [COCOLogic-V2: Identifying Logical Inconsistencies via Truly Hard-Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28194)
*David Steinmann,Antonia Wüst,Kristian Kersting,Wolfgang Stammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了COCOLogic-V2数据集，用于评估真实图像上的视觉归纳推理能力，涵盖一阶逻辑的子集。通过将样本分为正样本、近边界负样本和远边界负样本，实现了对模型可解释性的细粒度诊断。实验发现模型在区分正样本和远边界负样本时表现良好，但在近边界样本上失败，且感知噪声和规则诱导的搜索空间在少样本场景下带来额外挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释模型（如概念瓶颈模型和程序合成方法）通常在简单任务上评估，缺乏对真实图像复杂推理场景的探索。COCOLogic-V2旨在填补这一空白，为视觉归纳推理提供更具挑战性的基准。

Method: 构建以物体为中心的数据集COCOLogic-V2，覆盖一阶逻辑子集；设计正样本、近边界负样本和远边界负样本三类样本结构，用于细粒度诊断模型的可解释性和归因能力。

Result: 模型在区分正样本和远边界负样本时表现良好，但在近边界负样本上表现较差；感知噪声和规则诱导的大搜索空间在少样本设置下构成额外挑战。

Conclusion: 视觉归纳推理仍是一个开放性问题，COCOLogic-V2为在该方向推动方法进步提供了具体的基础。

Abstract: While interpretable models such as concept bottleneck models (CBMs) and program synthesis methods enable verification of model decisions, their evaluation is typically limited to simple tasks, leaving complex reasoning on real-world images largely unexplored. We introduce COCOLogic-V2, an object-centric dataset for visual inductive reasoning on real-world images covering a broad subset of first-order logic. By categorizing samples into positive variants, near-boundary (NB), and far-from-boundary (FB) negatives, COCOLogic-V2 enables fine-grained diagnosis of model accountability. Our evaluations show that models tend to separate positive and FB samples well but fail on NB samples, while perceptual noise and large rule-induced search spaces pose additional challenges in few-shot settings. Together, these results highlight that visual inductive reasoning remains an open challenge and COCOLogic-V2 provides a concrete foundation for advancing methods in this direction.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Value-Constrained Credit Assignment in Fully Delegated AI Cooperatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28217)
*Young Yoon,Jimin Kim,Soyeon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在完全委托的AI合作社中实现奖励分配的框架，其中人类由代理代表，这些代理在异构价值约束下贡献数据并参与模型更新。核心思想是仅对经过每个委托人的价值档案筛选后仍保持可接受的更新给予信用。该框架在遍历学习（TL）基础上结合了价值条件梯度过滤、在线边际贡献信号和累积收入结算。TL通过保留显式遍历和梯度路径，避免了聚合中心化分布式学习的质量损失，提供了比FedAvg式联邦学习更精细的归因基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI合作社中多主体数据贡献与模型更新的公平奖励分配问题，特别是在异构价值约束下如何筛选和归因贡献，以及如何在去中心化学习中实现更精细的贡献核算。

Method: 提出价值条件梯度过滤（仅对通过委托人价值档案筛选的更新给予信用）、在线边际贡献信号、累积收入结算机制，并基于遍历学习（TL）作为计算基底，利用其去中心化反向传播和显式梯度路径特性实现精细归因。

Result: TL相比FedAvg式联邦学习在归因精度上更优，避免了聚合中心化分布式学习的质量损失；框架能够实现基于价值约束的贡献筛选与信用分配。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI合作社中异构价值约束下的数据贡献奖励分配提供了完整的解决方案，其归因机制优于传统联邦学习方法，并与数据估值、联邦贡献估计、个性化联邦学习和多元对齐等方向形成对比与衔接。

Abstract: We propose a framework for reward allocation in fully delegated AI cooperatives where humans are represented by agents that contribute data and participate in model updates under heterogeneous value constraints. The key idea is to credit only those updates that remain admissible after screening them against each principal's value profile. We formulate value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate. TL is especially attractive here because it performs decentralized backpropagation without the quality loss associated with aggregation-centric distributed learning and, we argue, offers a finer attribution substrate than FedAvg-style federated learning by preserving explicit traversal and gradient paths. The framework is positioned against data valuation, federated contribution estimation, personalized federated learning, and pluralistic alignment.

</details>


### [83] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Transfer Learning for State Estimation in Lithium-Ion Batteries using the Single Particle Model with Electrolyte](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28220)
*Gift Modekwe,Qiugang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于迁移学习的SPMe-PINN框架，通过预训练-微调策略，在保持电化学一致性的前提下，显著减少训练时间并实现跨电池高效泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法（有限差分、有限体积、有限元）求解非线性PDE计算成本高，而PINN虽具可扩展性优势，但针对不同电池化学体系或工况从头训练SPMe-PINN收敛缓慢、计算开销大。

Method: 采用迁移学习框架：先预训练模型学习通用电化学动力学，再通过权重迁移、冻结选定层、微调剩余参数（包括估计关键电化学变量）的方式适配目标电池。

Result: 基于PyBaMM的验证表明，该方法能够准确预测电压，保持电化学一致性，同时减少训练时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的迁移学习策略能有效提升SPMe-PINN在不同电池间的泛化效率，降低训练成本。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs), including battery electrochemical models. They typically en-force conservation laws within the loss function to ensure physically consistent solutions. Tradi-tional numerical methods such as finite difference, finite volume, and finite element techniques, re-ly on discretization and can be computationally expensive for nonlinear systems. To address this challenge, PINNs offer improved scalability, particularly for reduced-order models like the single particle model with electrolyte (SPMe). The SPMe describes lithium-ion battery dynamics through coupled diffusion, transport, reaction kinetics, and voltage equations. Despite these advantages, training SPMe-based PINNs from scratch for different battery chemistries or operating conditions is demanding and often leads to slow convergence. To overcome this limitation, this work introduces a transfer learning framework for SPMe-PINNs. The model is first pretrained to learn general elec-trochemical dynamics and then adapted to a target battery by transferring weights, freezing se-lected layers, and fine tuning the remaining parameters, including estimating key electrochemical variables. Validation using PyBaMM demonstrates accurate voltage prediction, indicating that the proposed approach preserves electrochemical consistency while reducing training time and ena-bling efficient generalization across batteries.

</details>


### [84] [Disentangling Continuous-Time Latent Dynamics: Identifiability of Latent SDEs via Diffusion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28228)
*Yuanyuan Wang,Wenjie Wang,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间潜在随机微分方程（SDE）模型中的因果表示学习可识别性问题，利用环境引起的扩散协方差偏移，证明了在无漂移稀疏性假设下，通过两个具有坐标方向方差比不同的对角扩散机制，潜在坐标可被识别到排列和缩放程度，并提出了两阶段估计器用于潜在解缠和因果图恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间潜在因果模型已发展出强可识别性结果，但连续时间潜在随机微分方程模型的可识别性在很大程度上仍是开放问题，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 研究加性噪声潜在SDE模型，观测数据通过未知非线性微分同胚得到，具有共享漂移但环境特定扩散协方差。首先证明线性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck系统的可识别性，然后推广到一般加性噪声潜在SDE。在温和光滑性条件下，瞬时漂移-雅可比因果图可识别到相同排列。提出两阶段估计器用于潜在解缠和可选图恢复。

Result: 理论证明了两个具有坐标方向方差比不同的对角扩散机制能够识别潜在坐标（排列和缩放），无需任何漂移稀疏性假设。实验在合成系统上验证了预测的可识别性边界，并在Hardanger Bridge监测数据上展示了实际传感器轨迹的应用效果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了连续时间潜在SDE模型的可识别性理论，提出的方法在合成和真实数据上均有效，拓展了因果表示学习在连续时间动态系统中的应用。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time series has developed strong identifiability results in discrete-time latent causal models, but identifiability in continuous-time latent stochastic differential equation (SDE) models remains largely open. We address this gap using environment-induced shifts in diffusion covariance. We study additive-noise latent SDEs observed through an unknown nonlinear diffeomorphism, with shared drift but environment-specific diffusion covariance. We show that two diagonal diffusion regimes with pairwise distinct coordinate-wise variance ratios identify the latent coordinates up to permutation and scaling, without any sparsity assumption on the drift. We first prove this result for linear Ornstein--Uhlenbeck systems and then extend it to general additive-noise latent SDEs. Under mild smoothness, the instantaneous drift-Jacobian causal graph is identifiable up to the same permutation. We propose a two-stage estimator for latent disentanglement and optional graph recovery; experiments on synthetic systems confirm the predicted identifiability boundary, and an application to Hardanger Bridge monitoring data illustrates the approach on real sensor trajectories.

</details>


### [85] [How Width and Data Shape Generalization Scaling Laws in Quadratic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28242)
*Julius Girardin,Emanuele Troiani,Yizhou Xu,Vittorio Erba,Florent Krzakala,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两层神经网络中泛化误差如何随模型参数数量、样本数量及正则化变化，揭示了由数据谱结构控制的相图和不同的标度律机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于标度律的理论工作通常在固定特征、无限宽度或在线SGD的设定下研究泛化与数据量或计算量的关系，缺乏在特征学习模型中同时考虑可训练参数数量和样本数量的联合标度分析。

Method: 分析了一个二次型两层网络在有限样本设定下、使用结构化数据时的ℓ2正则化经验测试误差最小化问题，显式刻画了泛化误差作为样本数、模型宽度和正则化参数的函数。

Result: 发现了由目标谱结构控制的依赖于数据的幂律标度行为，以及随参数数量变化呈现的不同标度机制组成的相图，刻画了包括插值开始在内的机制间转变及其对泛化的影响。

Conclusion: 模型宽度和样本量对泛化误差的影响存在多个不同的标度区域，这些区域的转变由数据谱结构决定，为理解特征学习模型中的联合标度行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding how performance scales jointly with model size and data is a central problem in modern machine learning. Existing theoretical works on scaling laws typically describe generalization as a function of data or compute, often in fixed-feature or infinite-width regimes and for online SGD. Here, we instead study how generalization scales with the number of trainable parameters and the number of samples in a feature-learning model. We analyze $\ell_2$-regularized empirical test error minimization in a quadratic two-layer network in a finite-sample setting with structured data. This setting allows for an explicit characterization of the generalization error as a function of the number of samples, model width, and regularization. Our results reveal a phase diagram with distinct scaling regimes as the number of parameters varies. In particular, the generalization error follows data-dependent power laws controlled by the spectral structure of the target. We further characterize the transitions between regimes, including the onset of interpolation, and their impact on generalization.

</details>


### [86] [Parameter Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT) for Network Traffic Prediction via Dynamic Urban Congestion Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28274)
*Abdolazim Rezaei,Mehdi Sookhak,Mahboobeh Haghparast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数高效混合Transformer（PEHT）框架，通过将城市移动性和拥堵信息整合到基于Transformer的架构中，并引入LoRA减少可训练参数，实现更准确的蜂窝网络流量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态城市蜂窝网络中，网络流量预测对资源分配至关重要，但由于复杂的移动模式、拥堵动态和异构用户行为，预测仍面临挑战。

Method: PEHT将主要网络通信特征与次要城市移动特征分离，在Transformer编码器中引入低秩适配（LoRA）减少可训练参数，并通过多模态融合策略将外部移动性和拥堵特征注入解码器以改进流量预测。

Result: 在Telecom Italia Milan数据集和多个合成拥堵场景下，PEHT在RMSE、MAE和R²指标上均优于现有最先进基线模型。

Conclusion: PEHT框架有效提升了蜂窝网络流量预测精度，且参数效率高，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate network traffic prediction is a critical element for efficient resource allocation in dynamic urban cellular networks. However, prediction remains challenging because network demand is influenced by complex mobility patterns, congestion dynamics, and heterogeneous user behavior. This paper introduces the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT), a network traffic prediction framework that integrates urban mobility and congestion information into a Transformer-based architecture. PEHT separates primary network communication features from secondary urban mobility features and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into the Transformer encoder to reduce the number of trainable parameters while maintaining high predictive accuracy. A multimodal fusion strategy then injects external mobility and congestion features into the decoder to improve traffic forecasting. Experiments on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and multiple synthetic congestion scenarios show that PEHT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of RMSE, MAE, and $R^2$. The implementation is available in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [87] [Towards Automating Scientific Review with Google's Paper Assistant Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28277)
*Rajesh Jayaram,Drew Tyler,David Woodruff,Corinna Cortes,Yossi Matias,Vahab Mirrokni,Vincent Cohen-Addad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI辅助科学评审的四级分类体系，并介绍了PAT（论文辅助工具）——一个用于深度科学评审的智能体AI框架，该工具通过推理缩放技术实现34%的错误召回率提升，并在STOC和ICML会议上展示了预提交评审效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类同行评审无法匹配AI辅助科学产出的快速增长，因此需要部署AI来加速验证和评审过程。

Method: 提出了四个渐进级别的AI-人类协作评审分类体系；开发了PAT智能体框架，该框架能完整审阅科学稿件并检查理论结果、验证实验、提出改进建议、识别潜在缺陷，并利用推理缩放技术提升性能。

Result: 在SPOT基准测试中，数学错误检测的零样本召回率提升34%；在STOC和ICML的试点部署中能够识别关键错误并提出实质性改进建议。

Conclusion: 通过早期错误捕获，PAT能减轻审稿人的认知负担，同时保留人类对评审结果的控制权。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is driving a revolution in scientific discovery, accelerating everything from hypothesis generation to mathematical theorem proving. However, this rapid acceleration is creating a systemic challenge: traditional human peer review cannot scale to match the influx of AI-assisted science. Ultimately, to resolve this tension, we must also deploy AI to accelerate the verification and review process itself. To frame the discussion around this transition, we propose a taxonomy consisting of four progressive levels of AI-human collaboration in scientific evaluation, and discuss various trade-offs involved with each.
  As a step toward this future, we introduce the Paper Assistant Tool (PAT), an agentic AI framework built for deep scientific review and verification. PAT ingests full scientific manuscripts and produces a comprehensive evaluation, checking theoretical results, validating experiments, suggesting improvements, and identifying potential flaws. By utilizing inference scaling techniques, PAT is able to identify deeper issues than a single model call alone, achieving a 34% improvement over zero-shot recall on mathematical errors in the SPOT benchmark. Pilot deployments of PAT as a pre-submission tool for authors at two major Computer Science conferences -- STOC and ICML -- demonstrate its ability to identify critical errors and suggest substantive improvements to research papers. By catching errors early, PAT eases the cognitive burden placed on referees, while preserving their control over the outcomes of the review process.

</details>


### [88] [Democratic ICAI: Debating Our Way to Steering Principles from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28294)
*Kevin Kingslin,Anish Natekar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Savita Bhat,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Democratic ICAI 通过结构化人物辩论生成多角度的偏好解释，从而得到更清晰、更全面的引导原则，在创意偏好基准测试中提升了偏好预测的忠实度和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的对齐方法（如 Inverse Constitutional AI）虽然能将偏好总结为自然语言原则，但单次解释无法捕捉复杂决策中的细微差别，导致偏好理解不完整。

Method: 提出 Democratic ICAI，通过结构化人物辩论（structured persona debate）收集多个相互竞争的推理依据，从中提取更清晰全面的引导原则，并使用 LLM 和决策树两种评判器进行决策建模。

Result: 在 MuCE-Pref 和 LiTBench 创意偏好基准测试上，Democratic ICAI 相比 deliberative prompting 和基于原则的基线方法，平均偏好预测准确率更高，且生成的 constitution 更受 LLM 标注者偏好。

Conclusion: Democratic ICAI 能够产生更忠实于人类偏好的偏好结构，为偏好对齐中的可解释性和决策建模提供了更有效的路径。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment often struggles to capture the reasoning that underlies human judgments. Many evaluations rely on multiple interacting criteria, yet pairwise labels reveal only the final choice rather than the considerations that shape preferences. Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) improves interpretability in decision making by summarizing preferences into natural-language principles, but its single-pass explanations miss much of the nuance involved in complex decisions. We introduce Democratic ICAI, a novel approach that gathers multiple competing rationales through structured persona debate, offering a broader and more expressive account of the factors influencing each comparison. From these richer signals, we derive clearer and more comprehensive steering principles and use them to guide decision modeling through both LLM-based and decision-tree judges. Experiments on creative preference benchmarks, MuCE-Pref and LiTBench, across multiple creative task categories show that Democratic ICAI yields a more faithful preference structure. It improves average preference prediction across tasks relative to deliberative prompting and principle-based baselines, while producing constitutions that LLM annotators prefer.

</details>


### [89] [VGB for Masked Diffusion Model: Efficient Test-time Scaling for Reward Satisfaction and Sample Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28301)
*Kijung Jeon,Thuy-Duong Vuong,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDM-VGB，一种基于掩码扩散模型的有偏采样方法，通过在生成过程中引入奖励引导的重掩码机制，在满足结构约束的同时优化下游奖励。理论证明该方法对过程验证器噪声具有鲁棒性，且复杂度为二次方，优于最佳-of-N方法的指数复杂度；在数独和QM9等基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展是提升生成模型的有效范式，尤其在输出需满足结构约束或优化下游奖励的场景中。现有方法在约束满足和高奖励生成方面存在效率与鲁棒性不足的问题。

Method: 将Jerrum-Sinclair回溯马尔可夫链从固定前缀树扩展到掩码状态图，允许任意位置的解除掩码和重掩码操作。采样过程偏向于能产生更高价值部分配置的移动，实现高奖励生成与低奖励样本的高效修复。

Result: 理论证明MDM-VGB对过程验证器噪声鲁棒且复杂度为O(n²)，而best-of-N因误差累积呈指数复杂度。在数独和QM9等基准上取得强实证性能。

Conclusion: MDM-VGB将回溯思想推广到离散扩散模型的掩码状态空间，为约束满足和高奖励生成提供了理论上有原则、实践上高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling is a promising paradigm to improve generative models, especially when outputs must satisfy structural constraints or optimize downstream rewards. We consider Masked Diffusion Model (MDM) and introduce MDM-VGB, a discrete diffusion sampler that augments unmasking generation with theoretically principled reward-guided remasking. Inspired by the recent success of the classical Jerrum-Sinclair backtracking Markov chain in reward-tilted generation, MDM-VGB extends the backtracking random walk from a fixed prefix tree to a masked-state graph, allowing tokens to be unmasked and remasked at arbitrary positions. The resulting sampler favors unmasking and remasking moves that lead to higher-value partial configurations, enabling both effective high-reward generation and efficient repair of low-reward samples. We prove that MDM-VGB is robust to process-verifier noise and achieves quadratic complexity, while popular test-time heuristics such as best-of-$N$ can incur exponential complexity due to error accumulation. Our theoretical findings are corroborated by strong empirical performance, particularly on popular constraint-satisfaction and scientific benchmarks such as Sudoku and QM9.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Test-Input Generation for Tensor Programs: What Actually Finds Kernel Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27396)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了张量核测试输入生成的七种策略（基于26个算子语料库，含16个正确控制与10个LLM风格错误变体），发现边界形状采样在召回率（78%）与误报率（0%）间达到最优平衡，而对抗性值采样虽召回率更高（99%）但误报率飙升（94%），边界形状对softmax尾部掩码错误尤为有效（召回率从0%提升至100%/62%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量核的测试输入生成长期依赖经验性做法（如固定形状、数据类型和全等检查），缺乏系统评估与量化分析，需明确不同策略的优劣。

Method: 基于gpuemu算子模式感知种子模糊测试器，在RTX 3060 GPU上对26个算子语料库（16个正确控制+10个含记录转录模式的LLM风格错误变体）评估七种策略，策略变化包括形状候选集、数据类型组合和输入值分布，从错误召回率和控制误报率两维度报告结果。

Result: 边界形状采样以78%召回率和0%误报率胜出；对抗性值采样召回率99%但误报率达94%；对softmax尾部掩码错误，常规策略召回率0%，边界形状提升至100%和62%。

Conclusion: 边界形状采样是操作上安全的策略选择，能有效检测特定错误模式且不产生误报；不同策略在不同错误模式上表现差异显著，需针对性选择。

Abstract: Test-input generation for tensor kernels is folkloric. Most projects pick a representative shape and dtype, run a fixed-shape allclose-style check, and ship. We make the choices explicit and measure them. Using the gpuemu op-schema-aware seeded fuzzer (arXiv:2606.20128), we evaluate seven test-generation strategies across a 26-op corpus (16 correct controls and 10 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription patterns) on an RTX 3060 GPU instance. Strategies vary the shape candidate set, the dtype mix, and the input value distribution. We report each strategy on two axes: bug recall and control false-positive (FP) rate. Boundary-only shape sampling is the operationally safe winner: 78% recall on the 10 buggy kernels with 0% FP on the 16 controls. Adversarial value sampling reaches higher recall (99%) but inflates control FP to 94% because the strategy injects NaN and Inf inputs and the validator's NaN check fires on every kernel that propagates them, not only on buggy kernels. On the two softmax tail-mask bugs the "regular" strategy (no boundary shapes) catches 0%, while boundary raises recall to 100% and 62% respectively. That gap is the clearest single signal in the data. The corpus result is about which seeded bug patterns each strategy catches, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM.

</details>


### [91] [Implementing GenAI-Supported Learning in Software Engineering and Computer Science Education using Bloom's Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27398)
*Vahid Garousi,Zafar Jafarov,Aytan Mövsümova,Leyla Memmedova,Hüseyn Mirzayev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出与Bloom认知分类学对齐的生成式AI使用框架，通过多门SE/CS课程实证，发现明确的教学指导能引导学生将GenAI用于高阶认知活动（分析、评价、反思），并促进负责任的AI使用，为教育中GenAI的整合提供了可扩展的教学驱动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程教育中的应用带来学习支持机会，同时也引发表层学习和学术诚信方面的担忧，因此需要探索如何通过明确的教学指导来支持负责任的GenAI使用。

Method: 设计了与Bloom认知分类学对齐的GenAI框架，明确不同认知层级下GenAI的适当角色，将该框架嵌入Queen's University Belfast和Azerbaijan Technical University多门SE/CS课程的指令、实验和评估中，通过匿名问卷和学习制品收集数据，采用主题分析法并以Bloom分类学作为分析透镜。

Result: 学生认为GenAI对高阶认知活动（分析、评价、反思）最有价值，对基础学习则不太适用；明确的Bloom层级指导促使学生反思性地使用GenAI，在需要独立思考时会有意延迟或选择不使用GenAI；师生均报告了教学效益（高阶认知支持）与挑战（认知负荷和教学设计工作量）并存。

Conclusion: GenAI的教育价值在于认知学习目标、教学指导和学习者自我调节之间的有意对齐；Bloom分类学为SE/CS教育中负责任的GenAI使用提供了可扩展的、教学驱动的框架，是强制执行式应对方案之外的一种实用替代方案。

Abstract: Context: Generative AI adoption in software engineering education raises opportunities for learning support alongside concerns about superficial learning and academic integrity.
  Objective: This study investigates how explicit instructional guidance aligned with Bloom's taxonomy supports responsible GenAI use in SE/CS education, exploring student and instructor perceptions of GenAI-supported learning.
  Method: We designed a Bloom-aligned GenAI framework that articulated appropriate GenAI roles at different cognitive levels. The framework was embedded in course instructions, labs, and assessment across multiple SE/CS courses at Queen's University Belfast and Azerbaijan Technical University. Data were collected via anonymous questionnaires and learning artifacts, analyzed using thematic analysis with Bloom's taxonomy as an analytic lens.
  Results: Students perceived GenAI as most valuable for higher-order cognitive activities (analysis, evaluation, reflection) and less suitable for foundational learning. Explicit Bloom-level guidance influenced students to use GenAI reflectively, with delayed or intentional non-use when independent thinking was prioritized. Both students and instructors reported pedagogical benefits alongside challenges in cognitive effort and instructional design workload.
  Conclusion: GenAI's educational value lies in intentional alignment between cognitive learning goals, instructional guidance, and learner self-regulation. Bloom's taxonomy provides a scalable, pedagogy-driven framework for responsible GenAI use in SE/CS education, offering a practical alternative to enforcement-focused responses.

</details>


### [92] [Formal Grammars in Business Process Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27399)
*Milliam Maxime Zekeng Ndadji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对34篇关于形式文法与业务流程管理（BPM）交叉领域的研究进行了系统文献综述，识别出七个研究流：组织流程设计文法、基于BWW本体框架的流程建模语言文法评估、生产规则文法、属性文法、图文法、文法推断和过程代数。综述揭示了形式文法已影响BPM全生命周期，但七个研究流彼此并行发展，缺乏跨流合成，并提出了五个基于语料库的开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式文法在BPM领域已被广泛使用，但相关研究分散在不同方向，缺乏系统性的梳理与整合。本文旨在通过系统文献综述，识别各研究流的核心贡献、所用形式化方法及其局限性，并揭示跨流合成的潜力，以推动BPM领域更深入地利用文法理论。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，筛选了34篇形式文法与BPM交叉领域的主要研究，识别出七个研究流，并对每一流派的贡献、形式化方法及局限性进行综合和分析。

Result: 识别出七个研究流：(i) 组织流程设计的流程文法；(ii) 基于BWW本体框架评估流程建模语言；(iii) 流程结构规范与变体管理的生产规则文法；(iv) 工作流声明式规范与分布式执行的属性文法；(v) 流程模型转换、生成与语义分析的图文法；(vi) 流程挖掘与发现的文法推断；(vii) 行为规范与验证的过程代数。综述发现形式文法已覆盖BPM全生命周期，但七个流各自独立发展，缺乏交叉合成。

Conclusion: 形式文法对BPM各生命周期阶段均有重要影响，但各研究流之间缺乏统一的理论融合。文章提出五个基于语料库的开放挑战，并认为更深入、统一地利用文法理论对于推动BPM前沿具有重要前景。

Abstract: Business Process Management (BPM) is concerned with the systematic design, execution, monitoring, and improvement of business processes. Formal grammars have emerged as a particularly fruitful formalism for BPM, offering generative, declarative, and analytical capabilities that are uniquely well-suited to process-oriented concerns. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 34 primary studies at the intersection of formal grammars and BPM. We identify seven research streams: (i) process grammars for organizational process design; (ii) process modeling languages evaluated as grammars under the Bunge-Wand-Weber ontological framework; (iii) production-rule grammars for process structural specification and variant management; (iv) attribute grammars for the declarative specification and distributed execution of workflows; (v) graph grammars for the transformation, generation, and semantic analysis of process models; (vi) grammatical inference for process mining and discovery; and (vii) process algebras as grammar-like compositional frameworks for behavioral specification and verification. For each stream, we synthesize contributions, formalisms employed, and limitations. The review reveals that formal grammars have influenced BPM across every lifecycle phase (from organizational design to formal verification and data-driven discovery) yet the seven streams have developed largely in parallel, without cross-stream synthesis. We identify five corpus-grounded open challenges and argue that a deeper, unified exploitation of grammatical theory holds significant promise for advancing the state of the art in BPM.

</details>


### [93] [Recall Before Rerank: Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Large-Scale Code-to-Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27401)
*Leonardo Venuta,Francesco Tosoni,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了深度学习模型在大规模代码搜索引擎中第一阶段召回的效率、效果和可扩展性，发现现有模型在TB级代码库上存在精度和扩展性局限，并提出基于LLM的代码标准化和查询重写方案以提升低性能模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义代码搜索和克隆检测对软件开发、维护和复用至关重要，但现有深度学习模型在超大规模（TB级）代码库上的第一阶召回率表现未知，其精度、可扩展性和资源消耗可持续性有待评估。

Method: 跨多种编程语言和数据集对当代深度学习模型进行基准测试；提出基于LLM的代码标准化（code normalisation）和查询重写（query-rewriting）方案。

Result: 发现现有模型在TB级源代码集合上的精度和可扩展性存在关键局限；所提LLM-based方案在低性能模型上实现了显著的精度提升。

Conclusion: 质疑了当前代码专用LLM在跨数据集场景下的鲁棒性和资源受限部署的可持续性，并为构建可扩展、高效的代码检索系统提供了可行建议。

Abstract: Semantic code search and clone detection are essential for software development, maintenance, and reuse. This paper evaluates the effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability of contemporary deep learning models for first-stage recall in large-scale code-to-code search engines. Benchmarking across multiple programming languages and datasets reveals critical limits in the precision and scalability of these models on Terabyte-scale source-code collections. We present LLM-based code normalisation and query-rewriting schemes that yield significant gains in precision for lower-performing models. Our results question the sustainability of resource-constrained deployment and the assumed robustness of current code-specialised LLMs across datasets. We conclude with actionable insights for building scalable, efficient code-retrieval systems.

</details>


### [94] [Towards Evaluation of Implicit Software World Models in Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27406)
*Egor Bogomolov,Yaroslav Zharov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"软件世界模型"概念，通过预测执行资源（峰值内存、墙钟时间、性能分析输出）来评估AI模型对软件执行过程的理解，发现现有前沿模型表现不佳且行为脆弱，表明它们缺乏对代码执行而非代码编写的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码执行基准仅覆盖控制流这一单一维度，无法全面评估AI模型对软件行为（即"软件世界模型"）的理解。作者希望将评估轴扩展到执行资源维度，以更全面地衡量模型对软件执行过程的理解程度。

Method: 以SWE-bench Verified为数据源，构建了包含预测测试结果、异常类、峰值内存、墙钟时间以及方法和行粒度的排名性能分析输出的评估任务，测试了多个前沿模型在这些维度上的表现。

Result: 所有被测试的模型（包括前沿模型）在所有预测任务上均表现一般（modest performance），且行为脆弱（brittle behaviour），没有模型展现出对软件执行过程的扎实理解。

Conclusion: 当前AI模型在理解软件如何执行方面存在显著不足，它们擅长生成代码，但缺乏对代码运行时行为的真正理解，即"软件世界模型"的构建仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Software engineering, whether performed by humans or by AI agents, requires reasoning about how software behaves. We call the internal model that supports such reasoning the software world model, and view current code-execution benchmarks as covering one well-studied slice of it -- control flow. In this paper, we take a step toward a broader evaluation by shifting the observable axis to execution resources: alongside test outcome and exception class, we predict peak memory, wall-clock time, and ranked profiler outputs at method and line granularity. We use SWE-bench Verified as the source of data to hold the test close to real-world software engineering tasks. All tested models, frontier ones included, show modest performance and brittle behaviour, suggesting a notable lack of understanding of how software is executed, as opposed to how its source code is written.

</details>


### [95] [Speculative Refinement: A Hybrid Autoregressive Diffusion Decoding Strategy and Its Behavior Across Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27474)
*Aditi Gupta,Neel Mishra,Kushagra Trivedi,Pawan Kumar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过Speculative Refinement（SpecRef）这一混合生成方法，系统研究了自回归与扩散模型联合评估中的四个关键问题：代码基准测试混淆了结构与逻辑正确性、多阶段校正中的精炼张力现象、对数似然与生成式评估产生不同的模型排名、以及标准Python后处理对非自回归生成器的破坏性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对自回归与扩散模型混合生成系统的统一评估方法，现有评估协议可能掩盖不同生成范式的真实能力和缺陷。

Method: 提出SpecRef方法（利用熵引导的选择性掩码从AR草稿预热扩散模型），并在六个基准测试上使用三种评估协议（执行式pass@1、精确匹配、对数似然评分）进行系统分析。

Result: (1) 代码基准测试中提供语法脚手架可使准确率从接近0提升至20%以上；(2) 多阶段校正会破坏已正确token，暴露出单模型评估无法看到的基准测试饱和天花板；(3) 对数似然与生成式评估对同一模型对产生不同排名；(4) 标准Python后处理会静默破坏非AR生成器的代码评估。

Conclusion: 上述发现适用于任何多阶段或非自回归生成流程，指向更具诊断性的评估实践。

Abstract: How should we evaluate generation systems that combine autoregressive (AR) and diffusion decoding? We study this question through Speculative Refinement (SpecRef), a training-free hybrid method that warm-starts a masked diffusion language model from an AR draft using entropy-guided selective masking. Evaluating SpecRef across six benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, GSM8K, BBH, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag) with three distinct evaluation protocols (execution-based pass@1, exact-match, log-likelihood scoring), we surface several findings relevant beyond our specific system: (1) code benchmarks conflate structural discovery with logical correctness: providing a syntactic scaffold lifts accuracy from near zero to over 20% without changing the model, indicating that much of the baseline failure is structural; (2) a refinement tension phenomenon where multi-stage correction degrades already-correct tokens, exposing benchmark saturation ceilings invisible to single-model evaluation; (3) log-likelihood and generative evaluation produce different model rankings for the same model pair, suggesting they measure different capabilities; (4) standard Python post-processing silently breaks code evaluation for non-AR generators. These observations apply to any multi-stage or non-autoregressive generation pipeline and point toward more diagnostic evaluation practices.

</details>


### [96] [Contextual Associations Between Webpage Elements for Web Accessibility: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27506)
*Kishan Rakesh,Shiyi Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为屏幕阅读器用户从可访问性树中预测与目标元素上下文相关的周围元素，通过链接预测方法构建人类标注数据集并评估四种机器学习模型在两个启发式基线下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕阅读器用户在按元素列表导航网页时，常遇到如"Read more"这类在W3C规范下有效但孤立时无法理解的 accessible names。页面中存在使这些名称有意义的周围元素，但缺乏机制将它们与目标关联。尚无实证研究探讨如何选择哪些周围元素与目标上下文相关。

Method: 在35个网站上构建人类标注的上下文关联数据集（每个页面3名独立标注者），将每个页面表示为从可访问性树导出的图，并增强DOM和CSS的空间与语义特征。比较四种机器学习模型（MLP, GCN, GAT, SEAL）与两个启发式基线，采用留一网站交叉验证和预注册统计框架，评估指标为Hit@K和MRR。已完成5个网站的作者标注试点研究以建立流程和参数化功效模拟。

Result: 试点研究中，四种学习模型的Hit@10范围为0.16至0.85，两个启发式基线为0.08至0.30。最终结果将在计划实验和分析完成后报告。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了一个网页上下文关联的人工标注数据集、链接预测在可访问性树图上用于上下文选择的实证评估，以及跨网站泛化分析。

Abstract: [Context] Screen reader users navigating webpages by element list often encounter accessible names such as "Read more" that are valid under the W3C Accessible Name and Description Computation specification but uninterpretable in isolation. The surrounding elements that would make these names meaningful exist in the page but are not linked to the target by any mechanism. No prior work has empirically studied how to select which surrounding elements are contextually relevant to a given target. [Objective] This registered report investigates whether human-perceived contextual associations between webpage elements can be recovered from the accessibility tree using link prediction, and whether the learned associations generalize across websites. [Method] We will construct a dataset of human-annotated contextual associations on 35 websites, stratified across the Tranco top-million list, with three independent annotators per page. Each page is represented as a graph derived from its accessibility tree, augmented with spatial and semantic features from the DOM and CSS. We compare four machine learning models (MLP, GCN, GAT, and SEAL) against two heuristic baselines under leave-one-site-out cross-validation with a pre-registered statistical framework, using Hit@K and MRR. [Results] We have conducted a five-site author-annotated pilot study to establish the pipelines and parameterize the power simulation, with pilot Hit@10 ranging from 0.16 to 0.85 across four learned models and 0.08 to 0.30 across two heuristic baselines. The final results will be reported after the planned experiments and analyses are completed. [Conclusion] The study contributes a human-annotated dataset of contextual associations on webpages, an empirical evaluation of link prediction for context selection on accessibility-tree graphs, and a cross-site generalization analysis.

</details>


### [97] [Test Case Selection for Deep Neural Networks: A Replication Study on LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27601)
*Ali Asgari,Mitchell Olsthoorn,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对深度学习测试用例选择(TCS)技术在大语言模型代码模型上的有效性进行了大规模复现研究，发现基于视觉DNN的部分结论可推广至代码LLM，但TCS效果具有任务和模型依赖性，其中基于不确定性的特征在早期故障发现方面有效，而基于表示的特征在准确率估计方面更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TCS技术实证评估几乎仅关注基于视觉的DNN和数据集，不清楚先前结论是否能泛化到LLM代码模型。因此，本文在代码LLM背景下开展大规模复现研究，检验TCS技术的可推广性。

Method: 在克隆检测、漏洞检测和技术债务预测三项代码分类任务上，覆盖17个任务特定微调模型实例、7个预测特征和13种选择策略（含12种特征感知策略和随机采样基线），从准确率估计和早期故障发现两个维度评估效果。

Result: 仅有部分视觉DNN的发现可推广至代码LLM。基于不确定性的特征在早期故障发现方面有效，基于表示的特征在准确率估计方面更稳健。TCS效果在不同任务和模型间差异显著，具有上下文依赖性。

Conclusion: 本研究为TCS技术超越视觉深度学习领域的可复现性提供了经验证据，并为代码LLM的运维评估中TCS的使用提供了实践见解。

Abstract: Recently, test case selection (TCS) techniques have been explored to support the operational evaluation of deep neural networks (DNNs) under limited testing budgets, where labeling cost is a primary concern and uncovering model failures early is a key objective. Although prior studies report promising results, existing empirical evaluations focus almost exclusively on vision-based DNNs and datasets, leaving it unclear whether prior findings generalize to LLM code models. This paper presents a large-scale replication study of TCS techniques in the context of LLM code models. We re-examine established TCS strategies originally proposed for DNNs and complement them with statistical sampling strategies not previously evaluated for TCS. We assess their effectiveness on three code-related classification tasks: clone detection, vulnerability detection, and technical debt prediction. The study spans 17 task-specific fine-tuned model instances, 7 predictive features, and 13 selection strategies, including 12 feature-aware strategies and simple random sampling (SRS) as a feature-agnostic baseline. We evaluate performance along two dimensions: accuracy estimation and early failure discovery. The results indicate that only a subset of findings reported for vision-based DNNs generalize when TCS is applied to LLMs for code. In particular, uncertainty-based features are effective for early failure discovery, while representation-based features are more robust for accuracy estimation. At the same time, performance varies substantially across tasks and models, indicating that TCS effectiveness is context-dependent. Overall, this study provides empirical evidence on the replicability of TCS techniques beyond vision-based deep learning and offers insights into their use for the operational evaluation of LLMs for code.

</details>


### [98] [LLM-Assisted Model-Based GUI Testing for Vue.js Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27665)
*Tao Li,Chenhui Cui,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMVue框架，利用大语言模型从Vue.js源代码中自动生成页面转换图（PTG），实现基于模型的GUI测试。在10个开源项目上的评估表明，该方法生成的图具有高精确率和召回率，并且相比随机探索基线显著提升了测试覆盖率和探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vue.js是流行的现代Web应用框架，但其自动化测试可靠性保障日益重要。尽管基于模型的测试已在其他平台的GUI测试中成功应用，但在Vue.js上仍面临挑战：分布在路由配置和单文件组件中的转换候选需要具体化和规范化，形成可执行的页面转换图用于测试。

Method: 提出LLMVue框架，使用大语言模型（GPT-4o）从Vue.js源代码中推断组件层次结构和路由转换，将分布在多个单文件组件中的信息合并为统一的页面转换图（PTG）。

Result: 在10个GitHub开源Vue.js项目上评估，使用GPT-4o作为LLM骨干，构建的图具有高精确率和召回率，且图编辑距离低。LLMVue引导的测试相比随机探索基线，在相同时间约束下显著提高了覆盖率和探索效率。

Conclusion: 这是首个利用LLM进行Vue.js应用基于模型GUI测试的工作，通过源代码级别的PTG提取实现了有效的自动化测试。

Abstract: Vue.js is a popular framework for building modern web applications. As Vue.js functionality and tooling support grow, ensuring its reliability (through automated testing) is becoming increasingly important. Although model-based testing has been successfully used to automate graphical user interface (GUI) testing on other platforms, its application to Vue.js remains challenging: Transition candidates, which are spread across router configurations and single-file components (SFCs), must be concretized and normalized into an executable page transition graph (PTG) for testing. To address this, we propose the LLMVue framework, which uses a large language model (LLM) to generate a PTG from Vue.js source code. LLMVue infers component hierarchies and route transitions, merging them into a unified PTG across multiple SFCs. We evaluated LLMVue on a collection of ten open-source Vue.js projects from GitHub, using GPT-4o as the LLM backbone. The constructed graphs demonstrate high precision and recall, with low graph edit distance. LLMVue -guided testing also significantly improves the coverage and exploration efficiency, compared to a random exploration baseline (with the same time constraints). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first use of LLMs for model-based GUI testing of Vue.js applications using source-level PTG extraction.

</details>


### [99] [BashCoder-R1: Towards Robust and Explainable Bash Code Generation with Robustness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27733)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jia Xu,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jiajia Ma,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Zenghua Wang,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 BashCoder-R1 框架，通过持续预训练、长思维链监督微调和鲁棒性感知强化学习，在 Bash 脚本自动生成中同时提升了代码的可解释性和鲁棒性，在 952 个真实任务的 BashBench 基准上全面超越 DeepSeek-V3.2 等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在 Bash 脚本自动生成中存在两个相互关联的问题：不可审计的"黑箱"推理过程，以及生成代码存在严重的鲁棒性漏洞。这两个问题直接影响系统稳定性和安全性。

Method: BashCoder-R1 包含三阶段流水线：(1) 持续预训练 (CPT) 专门化模型对 Bash 范式的理解；(2) 长思维链监督微调 (L-CoT SFT) 在专家验证的推理-代码样本上训练，模拟主动风险感知思维；(3) 鲁棒性感知分组相对策略优化 (R-GRPO)，通过加权奖励优化语法正确性、鲁棒性（shellcheck）和格式正确性。

Result: 在 BashBench（952 个任务，773 单行/179 多行）上，单行/多行任务 SyntaxPass 达 100.00%/94.97%，RobustPass 达 95.99%/79.33%，FullRate 达 90.04%/73.18%。在 FullRate 上分别超越最强基线 DeepSeek-V3.2 (Reasoning) 37.82% 和 20.18%。

Conclusion: BashCoder-R1 在功能性、鲁棒性和清晰度的人工评估中均获得最高质量评分，证明该框架能有效兼顾 Bash 脚本生成的可解释性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bash scripts are the cornerstone of system administration and DevOps automation, where code quality directly impacts system stability and security. In automated Bash script generation using Large Language Models (LLMs), two interconnected failures emerge: unauditable "black box" reasoning and critical robustness vulnerabilities in generated code. To address both, we propose BashCoder-R1, a novel framework for robust and explainable Bash script generation. Our pipeline combines: (1) Continual Pre-training (CPT) to specialize the model on Bash paradigms; (2) Long Chain-of-Thought Supervised Fine-Tuning (L-CoT SFT) on expert-validated reasoning-and-code samples to emulate proactive risk-aware thinking; and (3) Robustness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (R-GRPO), a reinforcement learning phase optimizing a weighted reward for syntax correctness, robustness (via shellcheck), and format correctness. We evaluate on BashBench, a new benchmark of 952 real-world tasks (773 single-line, 179 multi-line). BashCoder-R1 achieves SyntaxPass (100.00%/94.97%), RobustWarnRate (4.01%/16.47%), RobustPass (95.99%/79.33%), FuncRate (93.01%/93.85%), and FullRate (90.04%/73.18%) for single-line/multi-line tasks, outperforming the strongest baseline DeepSeek-V3.2 (Reasoning) by 37.82% and 20.18% in FullRate. Human evaluation on Functionality, Robustness, and Clarity further confirms BashCoder-R1 achieves the highest quality ratings.

</details>


### [100] [UNICS: Multilingual Code Search via Unified Pseudocode and Contrastive Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27747)
*Ye Fan,Jidong Ge,Chuanyi Li,Liguo Huang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UNICS是一个基于两阶段训练策略的多语言代码搜索框架，第一阶段使用伪代码作为统一表示进行预训练以学习跨语言算法级逻辑，第二阶段采用多任务迁移学习策略，通过语义切片分解代码并结合难正样本挖掘与跨语言动态难负样本采样，在多语言和跨语言基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练模型在多语言代码搜索中存在三个主要问题：数据不平衡、跨语言语义干扰以及从统一表示（如AST或IR）中丢失关键信息；同时传统对比学习策略仅依赖简单的难负样本采样，忽略了通过挖掘难正样本来学习代码内在语义不变性的潜力。

Method: 提出两阶段训练策略：第一阶段在自行构建的新数据集上以伪代码作为统一表示进行预训练，保留完整语义保真度；第二阶段采用多任务迁移学习，将代码分解为语义切片（如API调用、函数体），并结合难正样本挖掘任务和跨语言动态难负样本采样。

Result: 在多个多语言和跨语言基准测试中取得最先进性能，尤其在面向低资源语言的零样本迁移任务中展现出优越的泛化能力和性能平衡性。

Conclusion: UNICS通过伪代码统一表示和两阶段训练策略有效解决了多语言代码搜索中的跨语言语义问题，验证了难正样本挖掘和动态难负样本采样的有效性。

Abstract: While pre-trained models have achieved remarkable success in code search, their multilingual capabilities remain a major hurdle, plagued by data imbalance, cross-lingual semantic interference, and the loss of critical information from existing unified representations like Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) or Intermediate Representations (IRs). Furthermore, conventional contrastive learning strategies often rely on simplistic hard negative sampling while overlooking the potential of mining hard positives to learn code's intrinsic semantic invariance. To address these challenges, we introduce UNICS, a framework for multilingual code search built on a two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, UNICS is pre-trained on a novel dataset we constructed, which uses pseudo-code as a unified representation to learn a cross-lingual, algorithm-level logic that preserves full semantic fidelity. The second stage employs a multi-task transfer learning strategy that adapts this general knowledge to specific languages by decomposing code into semantic slices (e.g., API calls, function bodies) and incorporating tasks for hard positive mining and cross-lingual dynamic hard negative sampling. Experimental results demonstrate that UNICS achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple multilingual and cross-lingual benchmarks, showcasing superior generalization and performance balance, especially in zero-shot transfer tasks to low-resource languages.

</details>


### [101] [Reasoning Beyond Prediction: From Data-Driven to Causal Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27960)
*Roberto Pietrantuono,Luca Giamattei,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文呼吁以因果推理为核心的人机合作新范式，让机器不仅自动化或预测，更能主动增强工程师在复杂软件开发中的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件工程面临日益增长的复杂性（AI驱动产品、云原生架构、嵌入式环境等），现有基于深度学习的协同工程方法虽能实现自动化和预测，但远未满足智能支持的需求。

Method: 提出基于因果推理的人机合作新范式，让机器通过因果关系增强工程师的推理，而非仅依赖模式学习或自动化。

Result: 当前深度学习驱动的协同工程方法在提供智能支持方面存在局限性。

Conclusion: 软件越智能，越需要以因果为驱动的智能支持范式，实现机器主动增强人类推理的人机合作。

Abstract: Software engineering is an intellectually demanding, creative discipline that juggles a web of interdependent tasks to design, build, and assure the quality of increasingly complex systems. As our expectations from software soar - with demands spanning AI-driven products, pervasively distributed and cloud-native architectures, and deeply embedded cyber-physical environments - its complexity steadily increases. In response, a new wave of co-engineering methods and tools, fueled by deep learning, has emerged to augment the process, enhancing automation and decision support. Yet, these advances remain far from delivering the kind of intelligent support that modern software development demands. We call for a new paradigm of human-machine cooperation: one where machines don't just automate routine tasks or predict from learned patterns, but actively amplify engineers' reasoning through the lens of causation. As software becomes smarter, a smarter support is needed.

</details>


### [102] [Evolution-Aware Regression Test Prioritization of ML-Enabled Systems Using Gradient-Based Behavior Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28037)
*Eunho Cho,Donghwan Shin,In-Young Ko*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GBV-PD方法，利用梯度行为向量空间实现ML系统演化中的定向回归测试优先级排序，无需运行演化模型即可预测测试用例损失变化方向，在分类与回归任务中优于非定向基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML组件在持续演化（重新训练、微调、优化）过程中，先前有效的测试结果可能失效；单次演化步骤对不同测试用例的影响方向不同，传统回归测试优先级排序缺乏方向性，无法区分性能提升与下降的测试用例。

Method: 对每个测试用例计算其在原始模型下的损失梯度，通过PCA降维得到梯度行为向量（GBV）；将演化模型的参数更新投影到同一PCA基上，利用对齐程度估计每个测试用例损失的增加或减少趋势，在优先级排序过程中无需运行演化模型。

Result: 在分类与回归任务的实证研究中，GBV-PD持续优于非定向基线方法，与全梯度参考方法竞争力相当，并通过可重用的GBV缓存机制在多次更新场景下展现出更优的时间和存储效率。

Conclusion: 行为空间思想可被操作化为实用高效的机制，适用于ML系统持续更新场景下的回归测试。

Abstract: The machine learning(ML) component of an ML-enabled system evolves through retraining, fine-tuning, and optimization, so previously valid test results may no longer hold. A single evolution step can worsen performance on some test cases while improving others, making regression test prioritization inherently directional. We present Gradient-based Behavior Vector-Parameter Delta(GBV-PD), the first approach to operationalize the behavior vector space for evolution-aware regression test prioritization. GBV-PD represents each test case as a gradient-based vector(GBV), a low-dimensional projection of its loss gradient under the original model. It then projects the observed parameter update of the evolved model onto the same PCA basis and uses the resulting alignment to estimate whether each test case's loss is likely to increase or decrease, without running the evolved model on test cases during prioritization. In an empirical study across classification and regression tasks, GBV-PD consistently outperformed non-directional baselines and remained competitive with a full-gradient reference, while offering better time and storage profiles for repeated updates via reusable GBV caching. These results show that behavior-space ideas can be operationalized into a practical and efficient mechanism for repeated-update regression testing of evolving ML-enabled systems.

</details>


### [103] [SBridge: Identifying Source-to-Binary Function Similarity via Cross-Domain Control Block Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28058)
*Heedong Yang,Jeongwoo Lee,Hajin Yun,Seunghoon Woo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SBridge提出了一种基于控制块（control block）的跨域函数匹配方法，用于在二进制中识别与源代码函数相似的函数，在约40%函数被内联的情况下，实现了75.13%的recall@1和80.98%的recall@5，显著优于现有方法（最高43.31% recall@1和50.2% recall@5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二进制中识别复用代码对安全分析（尤其是漏洞传播检测）至关重要。尽管二进制间比较可行，但使用源代码作为参考更实用（更易收集和分析）。然而，源代码与二进制表示之间存在显著差距（如函数内联），现有方法依赖字符串字面量或整体结构相似性，无法捕获细粒度代码行为，产生大量误报。

Method: SBridge的核心创新是基于控制块（control block）的函数匹配。它将函数分割为有意义的单元（如条件语句和循环），封装关键功能特征。控制块作为跨域表示，使得源代码与二进制之间能够进行精确的相似度度量，有效克服函数内联和剥离二进制带来的挑战。

Result: 在从BinKit收集的3,904个真实世界C/C++二进制上的实验中，尽管约40%的二进制函数被内联，SBridge仍达到了75.13%的recall@1和80.98%的recall@5，远超现有方法（最高43.31% recall@1和50.2% recall@5）。

Conclusion: SBridge通过控制块匹配方法有效解决了跨域（源代码-二进制）函数识别中的表示鸿沟问题，显著提升了内联场景下的匹配精度，优于现有技术。

Abstract: We present SBridge, a precise approach for identifying functions in binaries that are similar to the given source code functions. Identifying reused code in binaries is critical for security, particularly for detecting propagated vulnerabilities. Although binary-to-binary comparison is feasible, leveraging source code as the reference is more practical because source code is easier to collect and analyze directly without compilation. However, significant gaps between source and binary representations, including function inlining, create challenges in cross-domain function detection. Existing approaches primarily rely on string literals or structural similarities between entire functions, failing to capture detailed code behavior and generating many false alarms. SBridge addresses these limitations through a key innovation: control block-based function matching, which encapsulates essential functional features by segmenting functions into meaningful units such as conditionals and loops. Leveraging control blocks as a cross-domain representation, SBridge enables precise measurement of function similarity between source and binary code, effectively overcoming challenges posed by function inlining and stripped binaries. For evaluation, we collected 3,904 real-world C/C++ binaries from BinKit. In experiments identifying binary functions identical to input source functions, despite approximately 40% of binary functions being inlined, SBridge achieved 75.13% recall@1 and 80.98% recall@5, outperforming existing approaches, which achieved up to 43.31% recall@1 and 50.2% recall@

</details>


### [104] [The ARDoCo Tool Landscape: REST API, TraceView, and TraceViz for Architecture Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28064)
*Jan Keim,Dominik Fuchß,Sophie Corallo,Tobias Hey,Julian Winter,Kevin Feichtinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ARDoCo工具生态，包括REST API、TraceView前端和TraceViz IDE插件，用于自动化软件架构文档、模型和源代码之间的可追踪性链接恢复（TLR），并通过初步研究验证了TraceViz在IDE内可视化能提升开发者的理解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发中产生的架构文档（SAD）、架构模型（SAM）和源代码等制品之间存在重要关联，但自动化恢复这些制品间的可追踪性链接在实践中仍难以部署，亟需一个可访问的工具生态来降低TLR的使用门槛。

Method: ARDoCo REST API暴露了四条TLR流水线（SAD-SAM、SAM-Code、SAD-Code、SAD-SAM-Code），通过HTTP端点支持异步执行和缓存；TraceView提供基于浏览器的引导式向导和交互式多面板探索界面；TraceViz是一个VS Code扩展，在IDE中直接将可追踪性链接叠加在文档上。

Result: 三个组件均已公开部署并可用。针对TraceViz的IDE内可视化初步研究表明，它在软件理解任务中能提升开发者的理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工具生态使最先进的TLR技术对架构师、开发者和工具集成者变得可访问，促进了实际部署和应用。

Abstract: Context and Problem. Software development produces interrelated artifacts like software architecture documentation (SAD), software architecture models (SAMs), and source code, whose relationships are essential for maintenance and consistency checking. However, automatically recovering links between these artifacts (traceability link recovery (TLR)) remains difficult to deploy in practice. Method and Aim. We present an accessible tool landscape for ARDoCo's TLR approaches: the ARDoCo REST API exposes four TLR pipelines (SAD-SAM, SAM-Code, SAD-Code, and SAD-SAM-Code) via HTTP endpoints with asynchronous execution and caching; TraceView is a browser-based frontend with a guided wizard and interactive multi-panel exploration of recovered links and inconsistencies; and TraceViz, which is a VS Code extension that overlays trace links directly onto documentation in the IDE. Results and Conclusion. All three components are publicly deployed and usable. A preliminary study for TraceViz's in-IDE visualization confirmed that it improves developer comprehension during software understanding tasks. The tool landscape makes state-of-the-art TLR accessible to architects, developers, and tool integrators. Video. We provide a screencast of our ARDoCo Tool Landscape and how it is used here: https://youtu.be/IOTEPZQ3tVs

</details>


### [105] [How Humans, Bots, and Agents Communicate About Vulnerabilities in Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28125)
*Pien Rooijendijk,Christoph Treude,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文计划研究Pull Request中人类、机器人和编码代理如何通过显式标识符（如CVE/GHSA）和隐式安全语言（如"未授权访问"）进行漏洞沟通，并分析这些引用与代码漏洞、审查活动及结果的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注显式漏洞标识符，忽视了缺乏正式引用的漏洞讨论；同时机器人和编码代理在Pull Request中越来越常见，但不同账户类型如何沟通漏洞尚未被充分研究。

Method: 基于AIDev-pop数据集，分析PR标题、描述、审查评论、提交消息和时间线讨论中的显式漏洞引用和隐式安全信号，并探究这些引用与引入/修复的漏洞、审查活动及结果之间的关联。

Result: 该研究将贡献一项大规模的、关于现代软件开发中漏洞沟通实践的实证研究。

Conclusion: 通过同时分析显式和隐式漏洞引用，并区分人类、机器人和编码代理的沟通模式，本研究将填补现有漏洞沟通研究中的空白。

Abstract: Developers may reference vulnerabilities in pull request discussions through both explicit identifiers, such as CVEs or GHSAs, and implicit security-related language (e.g., "unauthorized access" or "SQL injection"). Prior work has primarily focused on explicit identifiers, potentially overlooking vulnerability discussions that lack formal references. Bots and coding agents are becoming more common in pull requests, raising new questions about how different accounts communicate about vulnerabilities. In this registered report, we describe our planned study of vulnerability communication in pull requests by humans, bots, and coding agents. Building on the AIDev-pop dataset, we analyze explicit vulnerability references and implicit security-related signals across pull request titles, descriptions, review comments, commit messages, and timeline discussions. We further investigate whether these references are associated with vulnerabilities introduced or fixed in the modified code and how they relate to pull request review activity and outcomes. This study contributes a large-scale empirical investigation of vulnerability communication practices in modern software development.

</details>


### [106] [CrossLangFuzzer: Differential Testing of Cross-Language JVM Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28132)
*Xiaotian Ma,Qiong Feng,Yongqiang Tian,Wei Song,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出首个面向JVM跨语言编译的差异测试框架CrossLangFuzzer，基于Kotlin中间表示合成跨语言测试程序，并采用7种变异算子增强测试多样性，在5个主流JVM编译器上发现32个已确认缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代JVM软件越来越多地集成多种编程语言（Java、Kotlin、Groovy、Scala等），跨语言编译中语义差异可能导致严重误编译，而现有编译器测试技术仅关注单一语言编译，缺乏针对跨语言场景的测试方法。

Method: 利用Kotlin编译器的统一中间表示（IR）合成跨语言测试程序，应用7种变异算子对生成的测试程序进行多样化处理，提升缺陷发现能力。

Result: 在最新版Kotlin、Groovy、Scala 3、Scala 2和Java编译器上共发现32个已确认缺陷（Kotlin 15个，Groovy 4个，Scala 3有7个，Scala 2有2个，Java 4个）。

Conclusion: 跨语言编译存在真实且严重的缺陷，CrossLangFuzzer作为首个跨语言JVM编译差异测试框架能有效发现这些缺陷，项目已开源。

Abstract: Modern JVM software increasingly integrates multiple programming languages, such as Java, Kotlin, Groovy, and Scala, within a single application. Supporting such interoperability requires JVM compilers to perform cross-language compilation while reconciling subtle semantic differences across language boundaries. Errors in this process can lead to critical miscompilations, yet existing compiler testing techniques focus exclusively on isolated, singlelanguage compilation. To address this gap, we present CrossLangFuzzer, the first differential testing framework for cross-language JVM compilation. CrossLangFuzzer leverages the Kotlin compiler's unified intermediate representation (IR) to synthesize cross-language test programs. It further applies seven mutation operators to diversify generated test programs and improve bug-finding capability. Evaluated on the latest versions of five major JVM compilers, CrossLangFuzzer uncovered 32 confirmed bugs, including 15 in Kotlin, 4 in Groovy, 7 in Scala 3, 2 in Scala 2, and 4 in Java. CrossLangFuzzer is open-source at https://github.com/XYZboom/CrossLangFuzzer

</details>


### [107] [Humanizing Automatically Generated Unit Test Suites with LLM-Based Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28229)
*Wendkûuni C. Ouédraogo,Yinghua Li,Xueqi Dang,Paweł Borsukiewicz,Lingfeng Bao,Anil Koyuncu,Jacques Klein,David Lo,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TestHumanizer，一种混合SBST+LLM方法，将LLM作为受控重构层应用于可编译的SBST测试套件，以提升可读性和开发者友好度，同时保持行为和编译有效性。在350个类上的实验表明，编译率达到88-98%，覆盖率仅下降1-2个百分点，86-95%的重构满足保真阈值，开发者研究证实了显著的可读性和采用意愿提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的测试生成工具（如EvoSuite）能生成可编译、高覆盖率的测试套件，但这些套件通常难以阅读和维护。LLM可以生成更自然的测试，但直接生成方式脆弱，编译率仅51-78%。需要一种能在保持SBST可靠性的同时提升测试可读性和可维护性的混合方法。

Method: 提出TestHumanizer混合方法：以EvoSuite生成的可编译测试套件为基础，使用LLM（gpt-4o和mistral-large-2407）作为受控重构层，在三种上下文配置（最小上下文、代码上下文、摘要上下文）下进行重构，改善命名、结构和开发者导向的清晰度，同时通过验证门控机制确保行为和编译有效性。

Result: 在Defects4J和SF110的350个类上，EvoSuite每类生成15个套件，共计31,500次重构。TestHumanizer编译率达88-98%，接近EvoSuite的100%基线；结构覆盖率大致保持，通常在1-2个百分点内；86-95%的重构满足复合忠实重构阈值；重构套件在预测可读性、控制流和认知复杂度、结构异味方面均有改善。摘要上下文设置提供最稳健的权衡，长代码中心提示更易产生幻觉导致的失败。开发者研究（30个类，444个测试方法）证实了可读性和采用意愿的显著提升（Wilcoxon p<0.01），评审者间一致性较高。

Conclusion: LLM最有效的角色不是作为独立的测试生成器，而是作为基于验证门控的精炼层，增强鲁棒的SBST输出。这种混合范式在保持SBST可靠性的同时，有效提升了测试套件的可读性和开发者友好度。

Abstract: Search-based test generation tools such as EvoSuite produce compilable and high-coverage unit tests at scale, but their suites are often hard to read and maintain. LLMs can generate more natural tests, yet direct generation remains brittle, with compilation rates of only 51-78% in our study. We introduce TestHumanizer, a hybrid SBST+LLM approach that uses LLMs as controlled refactoring layers over compilable SBST suites to improve naming, structure, and developer-oriented clarity while preserving behavior and compilation validity. We evaluate TestHumanizer on 350 classes from Defects4J and SF110. EvoSuite generates 15 suites per class, and each suite is refactored under three context configurations using gpt-4o and mistral-large-2407, yielding 31,500 refactorings. TestHumanizer reaches 88-98% compilation rates, close to EvoSuite's 100% baseline and clearly above direct LLM generation. Structural coverage is largely preserved, typically within 1-2 percentage points, and 86-95% of refactorings satisfy a composite faithful-refactoring threshold. Refactored suites also improve predicted readability, reduce control-flow and cognitive complexity, and mitigate structural smells. The summary-based setting offers the most robust trade-off, while long code-centric prompts are more prone to hallucination-induced failures. A developer study on 30 classes and 444 test methods confirms significant gains in perceived readability and willingness to adopt, with Wilcoxon p less than 0.01 and substantial inter-rater agreement. Overall, LLMs are most effective not as standalone generators but as validation-gated refinement layers over robust SBST outputs.

</details>


### [108] [Govern the Repository, Not the Agent: Measuring Ecosystem-Level Risk in AI-Native Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28235)
*Daniel Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI自主编程代理在协作开发中产生的"集成摩擦"问题。通过分析超过93万个代理编写的PR，发现约50%的集成摩擦变化来自仓库本身而非单个代理。代理贡献的仓库级摩擦集中度（ICC 0.30）是人工贡献（0.16）的近两倍，且这一差异在控制多种变量后依然存在。结论是风险是生态系统属性而非代理个体属性，AI原生软件应在生态系统层面而非代理个体层面进行度量和治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系孤立地测试单个代理在基准任务上的表现，但代理各自通过测试后，仓库仍会积累问题。作者质疑这是个体代理的问题还是仓库系统本身的问题，从而引出对集成摩擦的研究。

Method: 收集超过93万个代理编写的PR，测量"集成摩擦"（将贡献集成到被其他贡献者并发修改的代码库中的成本），并分析在控制贡献本身、作者、大小和代理等因素后，摩擦的变化中有多少保留在仓库层面。同时对比代理贡献与人工贡献的仓库级摩擦集中度。

Result: 约一半的集成摩擦变化在完全控制后仍保留在仓库层面。代理贡献的仓库级摩擦集中度（ICC=0.30）是人工贡献（ICC=0.16）的近两倍，该差异在控制代码库大小、年龄、任务形态、过程成熟度和合并路径后依然成立。

Conclusion: 风险是生态系统属性而非代理个体属性。AI原生软件不应在单个代理层面而应在生态系统层面进行度量和治理。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open and merge pull requests in shared repositories at scale, and the field evaluates them the way it has always evaluated components, one agent at a time, on isolated benchmark tasks. Yet agents that each pass their own tests still leave repositories that accumulate problems no single contribution accounts for. We ask whether this problem belongs to the individual agent or to the repository where it accumulates. We study integration friction, the cost of integrating a contribution into a codebase that other contributors are concurrently changing. Across more than 930,000 agent-authored pull requests, we measure how much of the variation in friction stays with the repository after the contribution, its author, its size, and its agent are accounted for. About half does, and it survives full controls. In the same repositories, agent-authored contributions concentrate this repository-level friction roughly twice as much as human ones (intraclass correlation 0.30 versus 0.16), a gap that holds after controlling for codebase size, age, task shape, process maturity, and merge path. The risk is a property of the ecosystem, not the agent. AI-native software is therefore better measured and governed at the ecosystem level than one agent at a time.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Drifting in the Future: Stabilizing Path Following Drifting on High-Latency Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27914)
*Frederik Werner,Till Heintzenberg,Markus Lienkamp,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于量产车的自主漂移控制框架，通过延迟补偿、改进控制和制动稳定，成功在量产跑车上实现了稳定漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前自主漂移演示仅限于具有瞬时扭矩传递和独立驱动轮的研究平台，其在存在执行器延迟和机械耦合轴的量产车辆上的适用性不确定。

Method: 设计预测器补偿动力总成延迟；开发改进的控制公式以适应更高执行延迟及驱动桥差速耦合；引入基于制动的速度稳定。

Result: 在量产跑车上实现稳定圆形和八字漂移，横向误差1.1米，侧滑角超调0.06弧度，执行器延迟超250ms，同时抑制振荡并保持稳定跟踪。

Conclusion: 自主漂移在量产车上可行，为传统控制失效场景下的高级安全系统开辟了路径。

Abstract: Autonomously controlling and handling a vehicle at and beyond its stability limit is a mathematically and computationally demanding task. Prior demonstrations of automated drifting have been limited to research platforms with instantaneous torque delivery and independently actuated wheels, leaving their applicability to production vehicles with actuator latencies and mechanically coupled axles uncertain. To overcome these issues, we design a predictor to compensate for powertrain delays, develop a revised control formulation to accommodate higher actuation latencies as well as a differential coupling on the driven axle, and introduce brake-based velocity stabilization. This paper presents the controller framework, the model extensions, and real-world experimental results. We observe that our controller enables a production sports car with a combustion engine to robustly sustain circular and figure-eight drifts, limiting lateral error to 1.1 m and sideslip overshoot to 0.06 rad despite actuator delays exceeding 250 ms, while mitigating oscillations and maintaining stable path and sideslip tracking. In conclusion, our results establish that autonomous drifting is feasible on production-ready vehicles, opening pathways to advanced safety systems capable of stabilizing cars in scenarios where traditional control fails.

</details>


### [110] [Support-Constrained RL Enables Real-World Policy Improvement without Real-World Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27475)
*Raymond Yu,William Huey,Mustafa Mukadam,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCORE是一种实到仿再到实的框架，通过在仿真中约束强化学习到基于真实数据预训练生成式策略的支持域内，实现了对灵巧操作策略的有效改进，成功率从37.8%提升至89.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界训练的机器人策略存在不精确、缓慢且对扰动脆弱的问题，而强化学习虽能改进策略但通常需要昂贵的真实世界训练。仿真训练成本低廉，但无约束的RL会利用接触和动力学不匹配，导致不安全行为无法迁移到硬件上。

Method: 提出Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE)框架，通过流引导（flow steering）将仿真中的RL约束在基于真实数据预训练的生成式策略的支持域内，限制动作空间到基础策略已能产生的动作，确保可迁移性同时最大化策略改进。

Result: 在8项真实世界灵巧多指机器人操作任务中，SCORE将平均成功率从37.8%提升至89.9%，最佳基线为59.5%，且完成任务所需步数比基础策略减少36.8%。

Conclusion: 当策略优化被适当约束时，仿真能够显著改进真实世界的操作策略，为实到仿再到实的策略改进提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robots trained on real world data tend to be imprecise, slow, and brittle to perturbations. Improving these policies with reinforcement learning (RL) is an appealing alternative, but this process often requires expensive training in the real world. Performing policy improvement in simulation instead provides a far cheaper alternative, but unconstrained RL in simulation can exploit contact and dynamics mismatches, resulting in unsafe behaviors that do not transfer to hardware. Common forms of regularization can furthermore limit improvement by overconstraining to an imperfect behavior prior. In this work, we propose Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE), a real-to-sim-to-real framework that constrains RL in simulation to the support of a generative policy pretrained on real data. We instantiate this constraint through flow steering, restricting SCORE to actions the base policy can already produce, which ensures transferable behaviors while maximizing policy improvement. Improving a policy with SCORE requires minimal effort: it learns from sparse rewards, avoids distillation, and leaves the base policy untouched. Across eight real-world dexterous multi-fingered robotic manipulation tasks, SCORE improves average success rate from 37.8% to 89.9%, compared to 59.5% for the best baseline, and reaches success in 36.8% fewer steps than the base policy. Ultimately, through extensive experiments and ablations, we show that simulation can substantially improve real-world manipulation policies when policy optimization is appropriately constrained, introducing a new paradigm for real-to-sim-to-real policy improvement. Videos and code are available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/score/.

</details>


### [111] [AO-ARC: Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Motion Planning with ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27495)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AO-ARC是一种任意时刻多机器人运动规划方法，通过将AO-x元算法与ARC自适应解耦方法结合，在保持渐近最优性的同时实现了与最先进可行性求解器相当的初始解速度，并在机器人数量增加时比现有任意时刻方法更快更可靠地收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRMP方法中，可行性求解器速度快但无法保证解质量，而任意时刻方法在大规模机器人场景下收敛速度慢且不可靠。需要一种既能快速获得初始解又能高效收敛到最优解的方法。

Method: AO-ARC将AO-x元算法应用于ARC方法：通过迭代调用ARC在受边界约束的MRMP实例上（以makespan为代价度量），利用ARC的自适应解耦特性，同时在机器人解耦过程中保持AO-x所需的一致代价边界。

Result: 在2D场景（不同协调复杂度）和3D机械臂场景上的实验表明，AO-ARC的初始解时间与最先进可行性MRMP求解器相当，且随着机器人数量增加，收敛速度和可靠性优于现有任意时刻MRMP方法。

Conclusion: AO-ARC理论证明了渐近最优性，实验验证了其在大规模多机器人场景下兼顾快速初始解与高效收敛的优势，适用于实际应用。

Abstract: We present AO-ARC, an anytime multi-robot motion planning (MRMP) method that achieves initial solution times on par with state-of-the-art MRMP feasibility solvers while converging faster and more reliably than existing anytime MRMP methods as the number of robots increases. AO-ARC adapts the AO-x meta-algorithm for converting feasibility solvers into anytime algorithms by iteratively calling the original ARC method on bounded MRMP instances under a makespan cost metric. This exploits the adaptive (de)coupling of ARC while maintaining the consistent cost bound across robot (de)compositions needed for AO-x. We provide theoretical analysis proving the asymptotic optimality properties of AO- ARC and conduct empirical evaluation on a set of 2D scenarios with different levels of coordination complexity and a 3D manipulator scenario representative of real-world applications.

</details>


### [112] [Spacecraft Fiducial Marker for Autonomous Rendezvous, Proximity Operations, and Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27566)
*Ravi Kumar Thakur,Matouš Vrba,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO
