### [138] [Kimball's Data Warehouse Architecture: Evaluating the Challenges of Conformed Data against the Inmon Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27571)
*Júlio Rocha,Salviano Soares,António Valente,Filipe Cabral Pinto*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文对比了数据仓库领域两位先驱Inmon和Kimball提出的经典架构（Inmon的“中心辐射式”架构与Kimball的数据总线/一致性维度框架），并从信息质量、系统质量、个人影响和组织影响四个维度进行了比较分析。研究表明二者并非对立关系，而是互补的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对快速增长的数据，组织需要重构存储框架以高效处理员工通过企业应用发出的查询请求，因此有必要研究Inmon和Kimball这两种经典数据仓库架构的差异与适用性。

Method: 通过文献回顾和比较分析方法，基于Ariyachandra和Watson的研究，从信息质量、系统质量、个人影响和组织影响四个关键维度对Inmon的“中心辐射式”架构与Kimball的数据总线架构（一致性维度）进行对比。

Result: 两种架构在四个关键维度上各有优劣，且二者并非对立关系。Kimball在1996年出版的《数据仓库工具包》一书中，Inmon为其撰写了高度赞誉的推荐语，表明两位学者彼此尊重对方的贡献。

Conclusion: 数据仓库架构的选择应根据组织具体需求而定，Inmon和Kimball的架构是互补而非互斥的方案，组织可从两者中汲取优势来设计最佳实践。

Abstract: In recent decades, driven by rapid data growth, organisations have faced the need to restructure their storage frameworks to efficiently handle queries requested by employees through available enterprise applications. Investigating this need involves examining the classic approaches of William H. Inmon, widely known as the father of Data Warehousing, and Ralph Kimball. Although both shared the same core concerns, Kimball later suggested an alternative architecture focused primarily on user needs. According to Ariyachandra and Watson, Inmon's "hub-and-spoke" architecture and Kimball's data bus framework featuring conformed dimensions stand out among alternative approaches. A comparison across these architectures highlights four key aspects: Information Quality, System Quality, Individual Impacts, and Organisational Impacts. Although Kimball and Inmon proposed contrasting solutions, they did not view each other as rivals. For instance, in one of the editions of the book "The Data Warehouse Toolkit", published by Kimball in 1996, the back cover features a note by Inmon stating that it is "one of the definitive books of our industry. If you take time to read only one professional book, make it this book.

</details>


### [139] [DiStash: A Disaggregated Multi-Stash Transactional Key-Value Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27979)
*Yiming Gao,Hieu Nguyen,Jun Li,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DiStash是一个跨异构存储池（DRAM、SSD、HDD、NVM）的分离式事务性键值存储系统，通过扩展FoundationDB实现，支持对多个存储池中的KV对进行原子性读写操作，并允许应用灵活配置存储池为临时或持久化存储，以及选择数据复制（包容性）或分级（排他性）策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV存储难以在跨不同类型存储池（如DRAM、SSD、HDD、NVM）的事务中保证数据一致性，容易出现竞态条件导致数据副本不一致，或因故障导致KV对丢失，应用逻辑复杂化。

Method: 基于FoundationDB扩展实现，提出分离式事务性KV存储架构，支持单事务跨多个异构存储池的读写操作；允许应用配置存储池为临时或持久化存储，并自主选择数据复制（包容性）或分级（排他性）策略。

Result: 通过微基准测试和eBay生产环境负载量化评估了设计权衡，并开源了实现代码。

Conclusion: DiStash有效解决了跨异构存储池的事务一致性问题，简化了应用逻辑，在性能和一致性之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: A stash is a storage medium such as Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM), Solid State Disk (SSD), Hard Disk Drive (HDD), or Non-Volatile Memory (NVM). This paper presents a disaggregated transactional key-value (KV) store, DiStash, that governs KVs cross pools of stash types. It enables an application to use a single transaction to read and write different copies of one or more key-value pair across the different pools of stashes. It simplifies the application logic by (a) preventing undesirable race conditions that may cause copies of data across different stash pools to reflect different values and/or (b) failures that may result in loss of key-value pairs. A configuration of DiStash may use a pool of stashes as either ephemeral or durable storage. The application dictates whether the content of its participating stashes are inclusive (replicated) or exclusive (tiered). We implement a DiStash by extending FoundationDB. We quantify the tradeoffs with its design decisions using microbenchmarks and eBay's production workload. We open source our implementation at https://github.com/ebay-USC/DiStash.

</details>


### [140] [Single and Multi Truth Data Fusion using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28062)
*Hira Beril Kucuk,Norman W Paton,Jiaoyan Chen,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）进行表格数据融合（真值发现）任务，实验表明LLM方法在单真值和多真值场景下均优于传统的无监督真值发现方法（如DART和LTM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据融合方法在处理多源冲突数据时存在局限，尤其是在单真值和多真值场景下的准确性和泛化能力方面。作者希望探索LLM在数据融合任务中的潜力，以超越传统无监督方法。

Method: 采用多种提示策略（包括领域相关/无关、零样本/单样本提示）在三个不同基准数据集上进行实验，涵盖单真值和多真值场景，与DART和LTM等传统方法进行对比。

Result: 基于LLM的方法在所有数据集上均优于传统无监督真值发现方法DART和LTM。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效应用于数据融合任务，为单真值和多真值场景提供更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Data fusion, also known as truth discovery, is a data integration problem that aims to determine the correct value or set of values for each attribute of an object when presented with potentially conflicting values from multiple sources. Data fusion tasks belong to two main categories: single-truth scenarios, where each attribute has only one correct value, and multi-truth scenarios, where multiple values can be valid simultaneously. This paper investigates the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in data fusion tasks for tabular data. Various prompting strategies, encompassing both single-truth and multi-truth scenarios, are investigated empirically. Domain-dependent, domain-independent, zero-shot and one-shot prompts are evaluated on three different benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that LLM-based approaches outperform traditional unsupervised truth discovery methods, such as DART and LTM, across all datasets. The codebase of this study has been made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Same Coeffect, Different Base: Connecting Two Dominant Approaches to Graded Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28042)
*Vilem Liepelt,Danielle Marshall,Dominic Orchard*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文建立了两种分级余效应类型系统（graded-base 与 linear-base）之间的双向翻译，证明了它们在类型、分级和操作语义上的保持性，从而回答了两种风格之间关系这一开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种分级余效应类型系统（graded-base 和 linear-base）在过去十年中独立发展，但两者之间的形式化关系一直是一个开放问题。理清这一关系有助于在不同系统间转移成果和思想，并为语言设计者提供更明智的设计选择。

Method: 作者给出了两种风格计算系统之间的双向翻译，并证明了这些翻译在类型、分级和操作语义上的保持性。

Result: 证明了相同的上下文依赖概念可以在两种风格中表达，两种系统之间可以建立形式化的桥梁。

Conclusion: graded-base 和 linear-base 两种分级余效应系统在表达能力上是等价的，可以相互转换，这为语言设计者和理论研究者提供了在两个系统间迁移结果和想法的理论基础。

Abstract: Graded types provide a way to augment a type system with fine-grained information, e.g., to track side effects or context dependence and resource use (called coeffects). Graded types for coeffects have found their way into languages such as Haskell, Idris, and Granule, enabling resourceful reasoning via coeffect analysis with varying levels of generality. Two separate lineages of graded coeffect system have emerged in the last decade: those in which coeffect annotations are pervasive, requiring annotations on function types (which we call graded-base) and those in which coeffects are added by way of a graded modal type operator atop linear types (which we call linear-base). The latter has its origins in Girard's Linear Logic which has been a rich humus for programming language research focused on resources, whereas the graded-base approach emerged in the mid-2010s, seeing rapid adoption in programming language theory and practice, e.g. in QTT and Linear Haskell. The relationship between these two styles has however remained an open question. We answer this question by giving translations between pairs of calculi of both lineages that we prove type-, grade- and operational-semantics preserving. We show that the same notions of context dependence can be expressed in either style, building a bridge between the two lineages that enables transfer of results and ideas, while helping language designers to make better informed choices.

</details>


### [142] [Prophecy-Based Automated Verification of Message-Passing Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28066)
*Takashi Nagatomi,Musashi Katsura,Naoki Kobayashi,Yusuke Matsushita,Ken Sakayori*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种将消息传递并发程序的正确性验证问题归约为约束Horn子句（CHC）求解的全自动方法，基于预言（prophecy）和时戳技术实现模块化编码，并证明归约的可靠性与完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发程序验证方法在处理消息传递模式时，往往难以同时实现模块化编码和因果依赖关系的精确捕获，缺乏既自动又完备的验证方案。

Method: 借鉴RustHorn的预言技术，将每个发送者通道表示为未来将要发送的值的列表；为捕获不同通道间的因果依赖关系，为消息附加时戳；将整个验证问题归约为CHC可满足性判断。

Result: 证明了该归约是可靠且完备的：程序无断言失败当且仅当对应的CHC系统可满足；实现了针对Rust类语言的原型验证器，实验证实了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为消息传递并发程序的自动化验证提供了理论上有保障、实践上可行的新途径。

Abstract: We propose a fully automated method for verifying functional correctness of message-passing concurrent programs by reducing verification problems to constrained Horn clause (CHC) solving. Inspired by RustHorn's prophecy-based technique, we represent each sender channel by a list of values to be sent over the channel in the future, which enables modular encoding of sender and receiver threads in CHCs. To capture causal dependencies between different channels, we further attach timestamps to messages. We prove that the resulting reduction is sound and complete: a program is free from assertion failures if and only if the corresponding system of CHCs is satisfiable. We have also implemented a prototype verifier for Rust-like programs and experimentally confirmed the effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Multi-Objective Molecular Generation with Frequency-Controlled Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27467)
*Elia Colleoni,Paolo Guida,Didier Barradas-Bautista,William Lafayette Roberts*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: SpectralMol是一种基于进化计算和傅里叶系数的分子生成算法，无需预训练即可实现多目标分子设计，在多个基准测试中表现优异，尤其在多参数优化任务上表现出色，并在ClpP靶向药物发现基准中生成更多对接命中数和更多样化的骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型的分子生成方法需要大量预训练、存在训练数据统计偏差，且生成化学结构的可解释性有限。

Method: 将化学结构表示为紧凑的傅里叶系数矩阵，投影到固定基上生成位置-wise潜在向量进行SELFIES解码；采用NSGA-II算法维护多样性并支持多目标优化；通过控制不同频率的傅里叶模式扰动来实现骨架级修饰与局部亚结构变异的分离。

Result: 在标准化基准测试中获得可比的综合性能，在多参数优化任务上表现最强；在ClpP靶向药物发现基准中，与基于强化学习的模型相比，在固定oracle调用预算下生成更多对接命中数和更多样化的骨架，同时保持竞争性的理化性质。

Conclusion: 频率控制的进化动力学为多目标分子设计提供了一条可解释、高效且无需预训练的途径。

Abstract: Molecule generation methods that leverage generative models have been successfully applied to drug discovery. However, they often require extensive pre-training, suffer statistical biases in the training data, and might suffer from limited interpretability of generated chemical structures. In this work, we introduce SpectralMol, an algorithm based on evolutionary computation that processes chemical structures as a compact matrix of Fourier coefficients, projected onto a fixed basis to generate position-wise latent vectors for SELFIES decoding. The NSGA-II algorithm enforces diversity and enable separate objective functions rather than collapsed objectives into a scalar reward. The quality of the algorithm was tested against standardized benchmarks. The results show comparable aggregate benchmark performance with a task-dependent profile: SpectralMol is strongest on several multi-parameter optimization tasks. The same benchmark was used to perform an ablation study to demonstrate the advantages of a structured latent matrix. Finally, method was tested on a realistic ClpP-targeted drug-discovery benchmark, comparing it with the reinforcement-learning-based model under a fixed oracle-call budget. SpectralMol generates more docking hits and more diverse scaffolds while maintaining competitive physicochemical properties. The representation adopted in this work can cleanly separates scaffold-level modifications from localized substructure variations, as the former occur with perturbations of low-frequency Fourier modes and the latter with perturbations of high-frequency Fourier modes. The results support the evidence that frequency-controlled evolutionary dynamics provide an interpretable, efficient, and training-free route to multi-objective molecular design.

</details>


### [144] [DE-2LS: Differential Evolution with Late-Stage local-search for Unconstrained Single-Objective Numerical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27762)
*Dikshit Chauhan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出DE-2LS框架，在RDEx基础上引入平滑的偏向利用分支率更新和受保护的坐标模式局部搜索，通过U-score评估进行消融实验选择最佳配置。实验表明，DE-2LS相比原始RDEx提升11.45%，相比其他CEC获奖算法提升34.43%，验证了精心设计的局部搜索策略能同时提升收敛速度和最终解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无约束单目标数值优化需要在全局探索、后期利用和函数评估效率之间取得平衡，现有方法在后期搜索阶段缺乏高效的局部精化机制。

Method: 在RDEx基础上引入两个改进：(1) 平滑的偏向利用分支率更新（EB-rate smoothing），在后期搜索阶段增强局部开发；(2) 受保护的坐标模式局部搜索（guarded coordinate-pattern local-search），作为预算感知的精化机制。通过分阶段消融研究确定最终算法配置，使用U-score联合评估解质量和收敛速度。

Result: DE-2LS相比原始RDEx的U-score从33602.0提升至37448.0（+11.45%）；与多个CEC获奖算法相比，DE-2LS以178966.5的U-score领先第二名34.43%。

Conclusion: 精心设计的后期局部搜索策略能同时提升算法的收敛速度和最终解质量，DE-2LS框架验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: Unconstrained single-objective numerical optimization requires a careful balance among global exploration, late-stage exploitation, and function-evaluation efficiency. This paper presents DE-2LS, a late-stage, local-search-enhanced differential evolution framework built on RDEx for unconstrained single-objective optimization with variable bounds. The proposed method preserves the original RDEx evolutionary search engine and introduces two conservative refinements: a smoothed exploitation-biased branch-rate update in the late search stage and a guarded coordinate-pattern local-search that serves as a budget-aware refinement mechanism. Since the considered setting is unconstrained apart from variable bounds, all selection and local-search acceptance decisions are based solely on objective values. To determine the final algorithm configuration, we conduct a staged ablation study by testing multiple settings of the EB-rate smoothing mechanism, the initial EB-rate, the standard-branch Gaussian sampling scale, the selection-pressure parameters, and the local-search coefficients. The final configuration is selected using a U-score-based evaluation that jointly reflects solution quality and convergence speed. Experimental results show that DE-2LS consistently improves the original RDEx in direct head-to-head comparison. In particular, DE-2LS increases the U-score from $33602.0$ to $37448.0$, corresponding to an improvement of $11.45\%$. Moreover, compared with several competitive and IEEE CEC-winning algorithms, DE-2LS achieves the best overall U-score of $178966.5$, outperforming the others by $34.43\%$. These results show that a carefully designed late-stage local-search strategy can improve both convergence speed and the final objective quality of the algorithm. The source code of DE-2LS is available at https://github.com/ChauhanDikshit?tab=repositories.

</details>


### [145] [DE-2LS: Differential Evolution with Lightweight Late Local Search for Constrained Numerical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27764)
*Dikshit Chauhan,Anupam Trivedi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出 DE-2LS，一种在 RDEx 框架上增加局部搜索的差分进化变体，仅在运行后期激活轻量坐标模式搜索，在保持速度优势的同时提升开发能力，最终取得最优 U-score 80968 和最佳总排名 48。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定函数评估预算下，约束单目标数值优化需要在可行性、目标收敛和计算效率之间谨慎平衡，现有方法在后期精细化搜索方面仍可改进。

Method: 基于 RDEx 框架，保留其变异、交叉算子、成功历史适应、存档机制、种群缩减和 ε 约束处理，新增轻量坐标模式局部搜索作为保护性抛光组件，仅在运行后期激活，使用少量评估预算，并通过考虑可行性的比较规则接受候选解。

Result: 消融实验确认最终配置取得最佳 U-score；与 RDEx 相比 U-score 提升 5.58%；四算法对比中取得最高总体 U-score 80968 和最佳总排名 48。

Conclusion: DE-2LS 增强了 RDEx 框架的开发能力，同时保持其速度优势，受控的后期精细化搜索比激进或过早的局部搜索更有效。

Abstract: Constrained single-objective numerical optimization requires a careful balance among feasibility, objective convergence, and computational efficiency under a fixed function-evaluation budget. This paper proposes DE-2LS, a late-stage, locally search-enhanced variant of differential evolution built on the RDEx framework. The proposed method preserves the original RDEx components, including mutation and crossover operators, success-history adaptation, archive mechanism, population-size reduction, and $ε$-based constraint handling. A lightweight coordinate-pattern local search is added as a guarded polishing component around the current best solution. It is activated only in the late stage of the run, uses a small evaluation budget, and accepts candidates through a feasibility-aware comparison rule. Ablation results show that the finalized DE-2LS configuration achieves the best U-score among all tested variants, confirming that controlled late-stage refinement is more effective than aggressive or premature local search. In the direct comparison with RDEx, DE-2LS achieves a 5.58\% gain in U-score. In the four-algorithm comparison, DE-2LS obtains the highest overall U-score of 80968 and the best total rank of 48 among RDEx, CL-SRDE, and UDE-III. These results indicate that DE-2LS improves the exploitation capability of the RDEx-based search framework while preserving its speed advantage under the combined speed-accuracy scoring criterion. The source code of DE-2LS is available at https://github.com/ChauhanDikshit?tab=repositories.

</details>


### [146] [MMAO: A Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer with Endogenous Resource Allocation for Continuous and Discrete Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28109)
*Jinliang Xu,Liping Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出代谢多智能体优化器(MMAO)，通过私有一公共代谢资源循环实现内源性自适应，在连续和离散优化问题上验证了其作为参数轻量、自校准优化框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元启发式算法依赖固定种群规模、人工选择的搜索尺度和外部参数控制模块，缺乏内源性自适应机制。

Method: 每个智能体携带内部能量、连续角色状态、运动/结构记忆和局部搜索历史；种群共享公共资源池。通过鲁棒进度尺度和近期成功统计将适应度改进转化为归一化代谢增益，闭环调控感知强度、搜索幅度、角色漂移、分支、剪枝、重生和精英再投资。连续域采用能量调控对称零阶探测和角色插值运动；离散域通过结构感知、局部路径改进、引导扰动和能量加权边重用实例化相同控制律。

Result: 在CEC2017子集(10D/30D, 20个种子)和5个TSPLIB实例(100次离散运行)上进行了可重复实验研究。

Conclusion: MMAO是一个参数轻量、自校准的优化框架，其核心创新在于跨异质搜索行为的代谢内源性资源分配，而非作为通用最优优化器。

Abstract: Traditional meta-heuristics often rely on fixed population sizes, manually chosen search scales, and externally attached parameter-control modules. This paper presents the \textit{Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer} (MMAO), a cross-domain optimization framework in which adaptation is derived endogenously from a private-public metabolic resource loop. Each agent carries internal energy, a continuous role state, motion or structural memory, and local search history, while the population shares a communal resource pool. Fitness improvements are converted into normalized metabolic gains through a robust progress scale and a recent success statistic; the same closed loop then regulates sensing intensity, search amplitude, role drift, branching, pruning, respawning, and elite reinvestment. In the continuous setting, MMAO uses energy-regulated symmetric zero-order probing and role-interpolated motion. In the discrete setting, the same control law is instantiated through structural sensing, local route improvement, guided perturbation, and energy-weighted edge reuse. The paper combines an implementation-faithful formulation with a reproducible experimental study on a CEC2017 subset (10D/30D, 20 seeds) and five TSPLIB instances (100 discrete runs in total). The current evidence supports MMAO primarily as a parameter-light, self-calibrating optimization framework whose main validated originality lies in metabolically endogenous resource allocation across heterogeneous search behaviors, rather than as a universally superior optimizer.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [HandMade: Spatial Prompting for Generative 3D Creation with Part-Labeled VR Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27738)
*Jialin Huang,Rana Hanocka,Ariel Shamir,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HandMade是一种结合VR 3D草绘与语言指令的开放域3D资产生成工作流，将带有部件标签的粗粒度3D草绘作为空间提示输入生成模型，通过多视图部件引导和结构化提示保留用户指定的空间布局，同时利用语言描述身份、材质、风格和局部细节。技术评估和用户研究均表明该方法优于纯文本和基于草绘的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D生成虽降低了创作门槛，但文本是弱接口，难以精确表达3D空间意图（如部件位置、空间关系、物体组织结构）。现有方法缺乏有效的空间指定手段。

Method: 提出HandMade工作流：1) 用户在VR中绘制带部件标签的3D粗草稿；2) 将分段VR笔触转换为多视图部件引导和结构化提示；3) 将粗草稿作为空间提示而非待重建几何，输入现有生成模型；4) 用户通过3D草绘指定布局和部件关系，通过语言指定身份、材质、风格和局部细节。

Result: 在20个多样例子的技术评估中，HandMade相比纯文本和基于草绘的基线方法，能更好地保留用户创建的空间结构。8名参与者的用户研究展示了用户如何利用3D草绘指定空间布局、利用语言指定身份材质细节，并在初始创作和后续修订中使用这些方式。

Conclusion: HandMade提出了一种将VR 3D草绘与语言相结合的空间引导3D生成交互范式，并实现了从界面到生成模型的有效管线，为空间引导的3D创作提供了新途径。

Abstract: Text-to-3D generation lowers the barrier to 3D content creation, but text alone is a weak interface for specifying spatial intent: where parts should be placed, how they relate, and how an object should be organized in 3D. We present HandMade, a workflow that combines VR 3D sketching and language for open-domain 3D asset generation. HandMade treats coarse, part-labeled 3D sketches not as incomplete geometry to reconstruct directly, but as spatial prompts for existing generative models. It converts segmented VR strokes into multi-view part guidance and structured prompts, allowing users to specify object layout and part relationships through 3D sketching while using language for identity, material, style, and local details. A technical evaluation shows that HandMade better preserves user-authored spatial scaffolds than text-only and sketch-based baselines on 20 varied examples. A user study with eight participants characterizes how users make use of 3D sketching for spatial layout and language for identity, materials, and details across initial authoring and subsequent revision. HandMade contributes an interaction paradigm and interface-to-generation pipeline for spatially guided 3D creation.

</details>


### [148] [Context-Aware Explanations for Spatialized Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28081)
*Wei Liu,John Wenskovitch,Chris North,Rebecca Faust*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CAPE是一个上下文感知的解释框架，利用文档语义和布局空间上下文生成自然语言解释，帮助用户理解文档布局中的空间关系（如聚类、子群、离群点和桥接文档），在用户研究中相比基线方法被认为更有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要总结文档内容或解释布局生成方式，对理解布局内部空间关系的支持有限，用户难以解读文档空间组织的含义及区域间关系。

Method: CAPE识别显著空间模式（聚类、子群、离群点、桥接文档），构建多层次上下文表示，引导LLM生成基于空间上下文的自然语言解释；支持AI引导概览和用户驱动探索，提供多粒度解释。

Result: 在新闻和学术文档布局上展示效果，通过控制用户研究对比关键词基线和纯内容LLM基线，空间上下文解释被认为比纯内容基线更有帮助。

Conclusion: 空间上下文的自然语言解释能有效提升用户对文档布局空间组织的理解，CAPE框架优于纯内容基线。

Abstract: Spatialized document layouts are widely used for exploratory analysis of text corpora, but interpreting the spatial organization of documents and the relationships between regions remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily summarize document content or explain how layouts are generated, providing limited support for understanding spatial relationships within the layout itself. We present CAPE, a context-aware explanation framework that generates natural-language explanations grounded in both document semantics and layout-derived spatial context. CAPE identifies salient spatial patterns (e.g., clusters, subgroups, outliers, and bridging documents) and constructs multi-level contextual representations to guide LLM-based explanation generation. It supports both AI-guided overview and user-driven exploration, with explanations available at multiple levels of detail. We demonstrate CAPE on news and scholarly document layouts and evaluate it in a controlled user study against keyword-based and content-only LLM baselines. Our results suggest that spatially grounded explanations are perceived as more helpful than content-only baselines for interpreting the spatial organization of document layouts.

</details>


### [149] [Typing Behavior in Human-LLM Interaction: Keystroke Dynamics Reveal Cognitive Effort During Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28090)
*Laura Schütz,Yousri Cherif,Clara Sayffaerth,Thomas Weber,Francesco Chiossi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过用户实验（N=36）探究了击键动态作为人机交互中用户心理努力和感知输出有用性的行为指标。结果表明，困难任务导致更多击键、更慢打字、更多停顿和更高工作负荷；设备类型影响较弱；击键动态能反映认知努力，但无法预测用户对LLM输出有用性的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM融入日常生活，了解用户如何与这些系统交互对于有效的人机协作至关重要。本研究旨在探索击键动态作为衡量用户在与LLM交互过程中心理努力和感知输出有用性的行为指标。

Method: 开展了一项包含36名参与者的用户研究，操控任务难度（简单vs.困难）和设备类型（桌面端vs.移动端），记录击键动态指标并使用NASA-TLX测量工作负荷。

Result: 困难任务显著增加了击键次数、降低了打字速度、增加了停顿时间和自报工作负荷。设备类型影响较弱，移动端使用略微减少了输入长度和打字速度。击键动态能捕捉认知努力差异，但无法预测用户对LLM输出有用性的感知。

Conclusion: 击键动态具有作为LLM提示过程中认知努力实时指标的潜力，但在捕捉感知协作成功方面存在局限性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly integrated into daily routines, understanding how users interact with these systems is crucial for effective human-AI collaboration. This work investigates keystroke dynamics as a behavioral measure of user mental effort and perceived output usefulness in human-LLM interaction. We conducted a user study (N = 36) to examine how task difficulty (easy vs. hard) and device type (desktop vs. mobile) influence typing behavior and workload (NASA-TLX) during interactions. Our results indicate that hard tasks led to significantly more keystrokes, slower typing, increased pauses, and higher self-reported workload. Device type had weaker effects, with mobile use slightly reducing input length and typing speed. While keystrokes captured differences in cognitive effort, they did not predict perceived LLM output usefulness. These findings highlight the potential of keystroke dynamics as real-time indicators of cognitive effort during LLM prompting, while also showing their limitations in capturing perceived collaboration success.

</details>


### [150] [Functional outcomes and naturalistic engagement with a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health (Ash)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28241)
*Kristen M. Van Swearingen,Thomas D. Hull,Karthik V. Sarma,Caitlin A. Stamatis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项针对1,284名真实用户的单臂观察性队列研究显示，使用心理健康对话AI Ash四周后，用户的生活满意度、人际关系满意度、睡眠质量、行为激活和工作联盟均有显著改善（效应量d=0.14-0.26），且未出现自我膨胀（夸大倾向）的负面效应；参与活跃度（活跃天数、总会话次数和总使用时长）正向预测改善效果，但用户消息数量无显著预测作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注心理健康AI对临床症状减轻的效果，缺乏对日常心理功能指标体系（如生活满意度、人际关系、睡眠质量等）的评估，也较少监测潜在危害（如自我感知膨胀）。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 单臂观察性队列研究，招募1,284名新用户，在基线和第4周完成应用内单条目心理功能指标（生活满意度、人际关系满意度、睡眠质量、行为激活）、工作联盟和夸大倾向（自我感知膨胀）测量。采用配对样本t检验分析个体内变化，ANCOVA检验参与度与结局的关联（控制基线水平）。

Result: 所有心理功能指标和工作联盟均显著改善（p<.001，d=0.14-0.26），夸大倾向无显著变化。活跃天数、总会话次数和总使用时长显著预测第4周心理功能和工作联盟（p≤.006，偏R²=0.58%-2.15%），用户消息数量无显著预测作用。

Conclusion: 基于证据的对话AI可能拓展心理健康支持至广泛心理功能领域，超越传统基于症状的评估框架。但需进一步随机对照试验验证因果效应。

Abstract: Background: Conversational AI chatbots designed for mental health may offer an accessible, scalable avenue for supporting psychological well-being, yet prior evaluations have largely focused on clinical symptom reduction rather than broader indicators of day-to-day functioning, and have rarely monitored for potential harms such as inflated self-perception.
  Objective: We examined within-person change in psychological functioning indicators among real-world users of Ash, a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health support, over the first four weeks of use, and whether these changes were associated with engagement metrics.
  Methods: In this single-arm observational cohort study, new users (n = 1,284) completed in-app single-item measures of psychological functioning (life satisfaction, relationship satisfaction, sleep quality, behavioral activation), working alliance, and grandiosity (inflated self-perception), at baseline and Week 4. Paired-sample t-tests examined within-person change; ANCOVAs tested engagement-outcome associations at Week 4, controlling for baseline.
  Results: At baseline, participants reported below-average life satisfaction and fair sleep quality. Significant within-person improvements emerged across all functioning indicators and working alliance (ps < .001; d = 0.14-0.26), with no change in grandiosity. Active days, total sessions, and total minutes consistently predicted Week 4 psychological functioning and working alliance (ps <= .006; partial R^2 range: 0.58-2.15%; controlling for baseline), whereas user message volume did not.
  Conclusion: Findings provide preliminary data for the potential of evidence-based conversational AI to extend mental health support for broad psychological functioning, extending the existing literature beyond symptom-based outcomes.

</details>
